# Translation review

Generated by `scripts/translate.mjs`. For each line, a native speaker should check the text in the "Used" column. To change a line, edit it in the language file directly; re-running the script keeps your edits.

| Opening Prayer | Okusaba okuggulawo |
| All together pray: | Bonna awamu basabe: |
| You expired Jesus, But the source of life gushed forth for souls, and the ocean of mercy opened up for the whole world O Fount of Life, unfathomable Divine Mercy, envelop the whole world and empty Yourself out upon us. | Waweddeko Yesu, Naye ensibuko y’obulamu yasanyuka eri emyoyo, n’ennyanja y’ekisa n’egulawo eri ensi yonna. O Fount of Life, ekisa kya Katonda ekitasobola kukomekkerezebwa, kubikka ensi yonna era weefumite ku ffe. |
| O Blood and Water | O Omusaayi n'amazzi |
| O Blood and Water which gushed forth from the Heart of Jesus as a fount of mercy for us, I trust in you! | Omusaayi n'amazzi ebyava mu mutima gwa Yesu ng'ensulo y'ekisa eri ffe, nkukkiririzaamu! |
| Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have Mercy on us and on the whole world. (Say 3 times) Amen. | Katonda omutukuvu, ow'amaanyi, atafa, Tusaasire naffe n'ensi yonna. (Kyogera emirundi esatu) Amiina. |
| Jesus, King of Mercy, I trust in you! | Yesu, Kabaka w'ekisa, nkukkiririzaamu! |
| Our Father & Hail Mary | Kitaffe n'Omukama Mary |
| Leader: Our Father… All together: Give us this day… | Omukulembeze: Kitaffe… Ffenna awamu: Tuwe leero… |
| Leader: Hail Mary… All together: Holy Mary… | Omukulembeze: Mweebale Mary… Ffenna awamu: Mariya omutukuvu… |
| The Apostles' Creed | Okukkiriza kw'Abatume |
| All together pray the Creed: | Mwenna musabire wamu okukkiriza: |
| I believe in God, the Father almighty, the creator of heaven and earth and in Jesus Christ, His only Son, Our Lord, who was conceived by the Holy Spirit, born of the Virgin Mary, suffered under Pontius Pilate, was crucified, died and was buried. He descended into hell. On the third day He rose again from the dead. He ascended into heaven and sits at the right hand of God, the Father almighty. From thence He shall come to judge the living and the dead. I believe in the Holy Spirit, the holy Catholic Church, the Communion of Saints, the forgiveness of sins, the resurrection of the body and life everlasting. Amen. | Nzikiriza mu Katonda, Kitaffe omuyinza w’ebintu byonna, eyatonda eggulu n’ensi ne mu Yesu Kristo, Omwana we yekka, Mukama waffe, eyazaalibwa Omwoyo Omutukuvu, eyazaalibwa mu Malyamu Omugole, n’abonaabona wansi wa Pontiyo Piraato, n’akomererwa ku musaalaba, n’afa era n’aziikibwa. Yagenda mu hell. Ku lunaku olwokusatu yazukira okuva mu bafu. Yalinnya mu ggulu era atudde ku mukono ogwa ddyo ogwa Katonda, Kitaffe omuyinza w’ebintu byonna. Okuva awo ajja kujja okusalira abalamu n’abafu omusango. Nzikiriza mu Omwoyo Omutukuvu, Ekkanisa Enkatolika entukuvu, Obumu bw’Abatukuvu, okuggibwa ebibi, okuzuukira kw’omubiri n’obulamu obutaggwaawo. Amiina. |
| On the Large Bead Before Each Decade | Ku Kkubo Ekkulu nga Buli Mwaka Gw'ekumi Gutandika |
| All together: Eternal Father, I offer you the Body and Blood, Soul and Divinity of Your dearly beloved Son, Our Lord Jesus Christ in atonement for our sins and those of the whole world. | Ffenna awamu: Kitaffe ow'emirembe, nkugabira Omubiri n'Omusaayi, Omwoyo n'Obwakatonda bw'Omwana wo omwagalwa ennyo, Mukama waffe Yesu Kristo mu kutegeera ebibi byaffe n'eby'ensi yonna. |
| On the Ten Small Beads of Each Decade | Ku Beedi Ekkumi Entono ez'Obukadde Bwonna |
| Leader: For the sake of His sorrowful passion All together: Have mercy on us and on the whole world. | Omukulembeze: Olw'okulumwa kw'omutima gwe Ffenna awamu: Tulage ekisa ggwe n'ensi yonna. |
| Concluding Prayer | Okusaba okukomekkereza |
| Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have mercy on us and on the whole world. (Say three times) Amen. | Katonda omutukuvu, Omuyinza omukulu, Oyo atafa, Tusaasire n'ensi yonna. (Kyogera emirundi esatu) Amiina. |
| Jesus, King of Mercy, I trust in you! (say 3 times) | Yesu, Kabaka w'ekisa, nkukkiririzaamu! (kigambe emirundi esatu) |
| Pray these prayers once a day for 9 days | Saba essaala zino omulundi gumu buli lunaku okumala ennaku 9 |
| How to Pray This Novena | Engeri y'okusaba mu Novena eno |
| Pray the same prayers each day, for 9 days in a row. | Saba essaala ze zimu buli lunaku, okumala ennaku 9 eziddiriŋŋana. |
| Opening | Okuggulawo |
| In the Name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit. Amen. | Mu linnya lya Kitaffe, n'ery'Omwana, n'ery'Omwoyo Omutukuvu. Amiina. |
| Litany of Our Lady of Perpetual Help | Olukalala lw'Omukyala waffe ow'Obuyambi obutaggwaawo |
| Lord, have mercy on us. Christ, have mercy on us. | Mukama, twasaasire. Kristu, twasaasire. |
| Lord, have mercy on us. Christ, hear us. Christ, graciously hear us. | Mukama, twasaasire. Kristo, tuwulire. Kristo, mu kisa otuwulire. |
| God the Father of Heaven, Have mercy on us. | Katonda Kitaffe ow'omu ggulu, Tusaasire. |
| God the Son, Redeemer of the world, Have mercy on us. | Katonda Omwana, Omununuzi w'ensi, Tusaasire. |
| God the Holy Ghost, Have mercy on us. | Katonda Omwoyo Omutukuvu, Tusaasire. |
| Holy Trinity, One God, Have mercy on us. | Katonda Omutukuvu, Katonda Omulala, Tusaasire. |
| Holy Mary, pray for us. | Malyamu Omutukuvu, tusabire. |
| Holy Mother of God, pray for us. | Maama waffe omutukuvu, tusabire. |
| Holy Virgin of virgins, pray for us. | Mukama w'abawala abatali bafumbo, tusabire. |
| Mother of Christ, pray for us. | Maama wa Kristo, tusabire. |
| Queen conceived without the stain of Original Sin, pray for us. | Kwiini yafuna olubuto nga talina kibi ky'ekibi ekisooka, tusabire. |
| Queen of the most Holy Rosary, pray for us. | Kwiini w'Essaala Entukuvu, tusabire. |
| Our Lady of Perpetual Help, pray for us. | Mukama waffe ow'obuyambi obutaggwaawo, tusabire. |
| O Mother of Perpetual Help, whose very name inspires confidence, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | O Maama w'Obuyambi obutaggwaawo, erinnya lye eryenyini erireeta obwesige, Jjangu onnyambe, O Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may love God with all my heart, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okwagala Katonda n'omutima gwange gwonna, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may in all things conform my will to that of thy Divine Son, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nnyinza okukola ebintu byonna nga nkwatagana n'ekyo ky'Omwana wo ow'Obwakatonda, Jjangu onnyambe, Omuzaala wange omwagalwa. |
| That I may always shun sin, the only real evil, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nnyinza okwewala ekibi, ekintu kyokka eky'obubi, Jjangu onnyambe, O Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may always remember my last end, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okujjukira bulijjo enkomerero yange eyasembayo, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama ow'okwagala. |
| That I may often and devoutly receive the Sacraments, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okufuna obulungi ekitambiro, Nnyamba nnyo, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may avoid every proximate occasion of sin, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa, Nsobole okwewala ekintu kyonna eky'ekibi. |
| That I may never neglect prayer, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole obutaleka kusaba, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may ever remember to invoke thee, particularly in time of temptation, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okujjukira okukusaba, naddala mu kiseera ky'okukemebwa, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may always be victorious in the hour of temptation, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okuwangula mu ssaawa y'okukemebwa, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may generously pardon my enemies, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okusonyiwa abalabe bange mu ngeri ey'ekisa, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama ow'okwagala. |
| That I may arise quickly, should I have the misfortune of falling into mortal sin, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okusituka amangu, bwe mba nga nfuna obuzibu bw'okugwa mu kibi eky'amaanyi, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may courageously resist the seductions of evil companions, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nze nsobole okuziyiza obulungi bwa banne ababi, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may be strong against my own inconstancy, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okubeera omunywevu nga nneerwanako, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama ow'okwagala. |
| That I may not delay my conversion from day to day, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nze nneme okukaluba mu kukyuka kwange okuva olunaku ku lunaku, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may labor zealously to eradicate my evil habits, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okukola n'amaanyi okumalawo emize gyange emibi, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may ever love to serve thee, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okukwagala nnyo okukuweereza, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may lead others to love and serve thee, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okukulembera abalala mu kwagala n'okukuweereza, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| That I may live and die in the friendship of God, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Nsobole okubeera mu mukwano gwa Katonda, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama ow'okwagala. |
| In all necessities of body and soul, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Mu byetaago byonna eby'omubiri n'emmeeme, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| In sickness and pain, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Mu bulwadde n'obulumi, Jjangu onnyambe, O Mukyala omwagalwa. |
| In poverty and distress, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Mu bwavu n'okubonaabona, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| In persecution and abandonment, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Mu kuyigganyizibwa n'okulekebwa, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| In grief and dereliction of mind, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Mu nnaku n'okweraliikirira, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| In time of war, famine and contagion, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Mu kiseera ky'olutalo, enjala n'obulwadde, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| In every danger of sin, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Mu buli kabi ak'ekibi, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When assailed by the evil spirits, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bwe nkwatibwa emyoyo emibi, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When tempted by the allurements of a deceitful world, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bw'okemebwa okukwegomba kw'ensi eno ey'obulimba, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When struggling against the inclinations of my corrupt nature, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bwe mba nga nneekalakaasa olw'obutali butuukirivu bwange, Nnyamba, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When tempted against the holy virtue of purity, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bwe tukemebwa okukola ekibi eky'obutali bulongoofu, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When death is nigh, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Okufa bwe kuba nga kutuuse, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When the loss of my senses shall warn me that my earthly career is at an end, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Obudde bw'okufiirwa obusobozi bwange bwe bunzijukiza nti omulimu gwange ogw'ensi guwedde, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama ow'okwagala. |
| When the thought of my approaching dissolution shall fill me with fear and terror, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bwe ndowooza ku kuggwaawo kwange okusembera, Nzijukira ntya era ne ntandika okutya ennyo, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When at the decisive hour of death, the evil spirit will endeavor to plunge my soul into despair, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bwe mba mu kaseera akazibu ennyo, omwoyo omubi ajja kugezaako okunzisaamu essuubi, Jjangu onnyambe, Omuzaala wange ow'okwagala. |
| When the priest of God shall give me his last absolution and his last blessing, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bw'anaaba nga omusumba wa Katonda ampa okusonyiyibwa kwe okusembayo n'omukisa gwe ogusembayo, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama ow'okwagala. |
| When my friends and relations, surrounding my bed, moved with compassion, shall invoke thy clemency on my behalf, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bwe bannange n'ab'emikwano, nga batudde okumpi n'ekitanda kyange, bwe banaaba nga bansabira ekisa kyo, Jjangu onnyambe, O Mukyala omwagalwa. |
| When the world will vanish from my sight, and my heart will cease to beat, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ensi bw'eneebula okuva mu maaso gange, n'omutima gwange ne guva ku kukuba, Jjangu onnyambe, O Mukyala omwagalwa. |
| When I shall yield my soul into the hands of its Creator, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bwe ndiwaayo emmeeme yange mu mikono gy'Omutonzi waayo, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When my soul will appear before its Sovereign Judge, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Omwoyo gwange bwe gunaaba nga gujja okuwaayo mu maaso g'omulamuzi waagwo, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When the irrevocable sentence will be pronounced, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Ekigambo ekitakyuka bwe kinaaba nga kiwuliddwa, Jjangu onnyambe, ggwe Maama omwagalwa. |
| When I will be suffering in Purgatory, and sighing for the vision of God, Come to my aid, O loving Mother. | Bwe ndiba nga nnyigirizibwa mu Purgatori, nga nneegomba okulaba Katonda, Jjangu onnyambe, Omuzaala omwagalwa. |
| Lamb of God, Who takest away the sins of the world, Spare us, O Lord. | Omwana gw'endiga, ggwe atwala ebibi by'ensi, Tusonyiwe, ai Mukama. |
| Lamb of God, Who takest away the sins of the world, Graciously hear us, O Lord. | Omwana gw'Endiga, Oyo atwala ebibi by'ensi, Mu kisa otuwulire, Mukama. |
| Lamb of God, Who takest away the sins of the world, Have mercy on us. | Omwana gw'endiga owa Katonda, Atwala ebibi by'ensi, Tusaasire. |
| Let Us Pray | Ka tusabe |
| O Almighty and merciful God, Who, in order to succor the human race, hast willed the Blessed Virgin Mary to become the Mother of Thy only-begotten Son, grant, we beseech Thee, that by her intercession we may avoid the contagion of sin and serve Thee with a pure heart, through the same Christ Our Lord. | Katonda Omuyinza w'ebintu byonna era ow'ekisa, Ayagala okuyamba abantu, yasalawo Omukama omulungi Maria afuuke Maama w'Omwana wo omu yekka, tumusaba atuyambe okuyita mu kusabira kwe tulyoke twewale ekibi n'okukola ebirungi nga tukozesa omutima ogw'amazima, okuyita mu Kristo Mukama waffe. |
| Amen. | Amen. |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Perpetual Help | Okusaba eri Omukyala waffe ow'Obuyambi obutaggwaawo |
| O Mother of Perpetual Help, grant that I may ever invoke your powerful name, the protection of the living and the salvation of the dying. | O Maama w'Obuyambi obutaggwaawo, mpa omukisa nsobole okusaba erinnya lyo ery'amaanyi, obukuumi eri abalamu n'okulokola abafa. |
| Purest Mary, let your name henceforth be ever on my lips. Delay not, Blessed Lady, to rescue me whenever I call on you. | Mariya omulongoofu, erinnya lyo okuva kati libeere ku mimwa gyange bulijjo. Tolemererwa, Mukyala Omusanyufu, okunzizaamu amaanyi buli lwe nkuyita. |
| In my temptations, in my needs, I will never cease to call on you, ever repeating your sacred name, Mary, Mary. | Mu kugezesebwa kwange, mu byetaago byange, sijja kulekera awo kukusaba, nga nkyusa erinnya lyo ery'ekitiibwa, Mariya, Mariya. |
| What a consolation, what sweetness, what confidence fills my soul when I utter your sacred name or even only think of you! I thank the Lord for having given you so sweet, so powerful, so lovely a name. | Kino kya kuzzaamu amaanyi, obulungi bwe, okwesiga okujjuza emmeeme yange nga nnyumya erinnya lyo ettukuvu oba ne bw’oba ng’okulowoozaako! Nneebaza Mukama olw’okuwa erinnya eryo ekkiriza, ery’amaanyi era ery’obulungi. |
| But I will not be content with merely uttering your name. Let my love for you prompt me ever to hail you Mother of Perpetual Help. Mother of Perpetual Help, pray for me and grant me the favor I confidently ask of you | Naye sijja kumatira na kwogera erinnya lyo kyokka. Okwagala kwange okukuliiko kukole nga kunziza bulijjo okukuwa ekitiibwa, Maama w’Obuyambi Obutaggwaawo. Maama w’Obuyambi Obutaggwaawo, nsabire era onnyambe mu byonna bye nkusaba n’obwesige |
| (mention request here…) | (ogamba okusaba wano…) |
| Hail Mary (three times) | Hail Mary (emirundi esatu) |
| Closing | Okuggalawo |
| From Good Friday to the Saturday before Divine Mercy Sunday | Okuva ku Good Friday okutuuka ku Lwomukaaga nga olunaku lwa Divine Mercy Sunday terunnatuuka |
| Pray these prayers each day, for 9 days in a row, followed by the Divine Mercy Chaplet. | Saba essaala zino buli lunaku okumala ennaku 9 eziddiring'ana, oluvannyuma ogoberere Chapleti ya Divine Mercy. |
| The Words of Jesus | Ebigambo bya Yesu |
| “Today bring to Me all mankind, especially all sinners, and immerse them in the ocean of My mercy. In this way you will console Me in the bitter grief into which the loss of souls plunges Me.” | “Leero ntegeeza abantu bonna, naddala aboonoonyi bonna, era mubateekemu mu nnyanja y’obusaasizi bwange. Mu ngeri eno mujja kunyamba okunzizaamu amaanyi mu bulumi obw’amaanyi obuva ku kufiirwa emyoyo.” |
| Prayer | Okusaba |
| Most Merciful Jesus, whose very nature it is to have compassion on us and to forgive us, do not look upon our sins but upon our trust which we place in Your infinite goodness. Receive us all into the abode of Your Most Compassionate Heart, and never let us escape from It. We beg this of You by Your love which unites You to the Father and the Holy Spirit. | Yesu Omusaasizi Ennyo, ow’obulungi obutaggwaawo era asonyiwa ffe, tolabira ku bibi byaffe naye olaba ku bwesige bwe twateeka mu bulungi bwo obutaggwaawo. Tufunire ffenna mu maka g’Omutima Gwo ogw’Obusaasizi Ennyo, era totuleka kugivaamu. Tukusaba kino mu kwagala kwo okutukwataganya ne Kitaffe n’Omwoyo Omutukuvu. |
| Eternal Father, turn Your merciful gaze upon all mankind and especially upon poor sinners, all enfolded in the Most Compassionate Heart of Jesus. For the sake of His sorrowful Passion show us Your mercy, that we may praise the omnipotence of Your mercy for ever and ever. Amen. | Kitaffe ow’obulamu obutaggwaawo, kyusa amaaso go ag’ekisa ku bantu bonna naddala aboonoonyi abavu, bonna nga bali mu mutima gwa Yesu ogw’ekisa ennyo. Ku lw’okubonaabona kwe okulungi, tuwe ekisa kyo, tusobole okutendereza obuyinza bw’ekisa kyo emirembe gyonna. Amiina. |
| The Divine Mercy Chaplet | Essaala ya Divine Mercy Chaplet |
| Pray the Divine Mercy Chaplet. You can use ordinary Rosary beads. | Saba Chaplet ya Divine Mercy. Osobola okukozesa obusale obwa bulijjo obwa Rosary. |
| 1. Make the Sign of the Cross. | 1. Kola Akabonero k'Omusalaba. |
| 2. Pray the optional opening prayers (below). | 2. Saba essaala eziggulawo (wansi). |
| 3. On the first beads above the crucifix, pray one Our Father, one Hail Mary and the Apostles’ Creed. | 3. Ku mpeke ezisooka waggulu wa crucifix, saba Our Father omu, Hail Mary omu n'okukkiriza kw'Abatume. |
| 4. On each Our Father bead, say: Eternal Father, I offer Thee the Body and Blood, Soul and Divinity of Thy dearly beloved Son, Our Lord Jesus Christ, in atonement for our sins and those of the whole world. | 4. Ku buli kintu ekikozesebwa mu kusaba, ogamba nti: Kitaffe ow’Obulamu obutaggwawo, nkuleetera Omubiri n’Omusaayi, Obulamu n’Obwakatonda bw’Omwana wo omwagalwa ennyo, Mukama waffe Yesu Kristo, okusonyiyira ebibi byaffe n’eby’ensi yonna. |
| 5. On each of the ten Hail Mary beads, say: For the sake of His sorrowful Passion, have mercy on us and on the whole world. | 5. Ku buli kamu ku buwero ekkumi obwa Hail Mary, yogera nti: Olw'okulumwa kwe okungi, tulage ekisa ffe n'ensi yonna. |
| 6. Repeat steps 4 and 5 for all five decades. | 6. Ddamu emitendera 4 ne 5 ku buli myaka ataano gyonna. |
| 7. Conclude with the Holy God prayer, three times: Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, have mercy on us and on the whole world. | 7. Okomekkereza n'okusaba kwa Katonda Omutukuvu, emirundi esatu: Katonda Omutukuvu, Omuyinza Omutukuvu, Atafa Omutukuvu, otusaasire awamu n'ensi yonna. |
| 8. Pray the optional closing prayers (below). | 8. Saba essaala eziggalawo eza bulijjo (wammanga). |
| Optional Opening Prayers | Essaala ez'okuggulawo eza kyeyagalire |
| St. Faustina’s Prayer for Sinners | Okusaba kwa St. Faustina eri Aboonoonyi |
| O Jesus, eternal Truth, our Life, I call upon You and I beg Your mercy for poor sinners. O sweetest Heart of my Lord, full of pity and unfathomable mercy, I plead with You for poor sinners. O Most Sacred Heart, Fount of Mercy from which gush forth rays of inconceivable graces upon the entire human race, I beg of You light for poor sinners. O Jesus, be mindful of Your own bitter Passion and do not permit the loss of souls redeemed at so dear a price of Your most precious Blood. O Jesus, when I consider the great price of Your Blood, I rejoice at its immensity, for one drop alone would have been enough for the salvation of all sinners. Although sin is an abyss of wickedness and ingratitude, the price paid for us can never be equaled. Therefore, let every soul trust in the Passion of the Lord, and place its hope in His mercy. God will not deny His mercy to anyone. Heaven and earth may change, but God’s mercy will never be exhausted. Oh, what immense joy burns in my heart when I contemplate Your incomprehensible goodness, O Jesus! I desire to bring all sinners to Your feet that they may glorify Your mercy throughout endless ages (Diary of Saint Maria Faustina Kowalska, 72). | O Yesu, Amazima agataggwaawo, Obulamu bwaffe, nkuyita era nsaba ekisa kyo eri aboonoonyi abavu. Omutima ogw’amaanyi ogwa Mukama wange, ogujjudde obusaasizi n’ekisa ekitagambika, nkusaba okubaako ky’okolera aboonoonyi abavu. Omutima Ogutukuvu ennyo, Ensibuko y’Ekisa mwe muva ebikadde by’obulungi obutayinza kukkirizika ku bantu bonna, nsaba okufuna omusana gw’okubalabirira. O Yesu, jjukira Okufa kwo okw’obulumi era tokkiriza kufiirwa baamala okununulwa ku bbeeyi ey’omuwendo ogw’Omusaayi gwo ogw’omuwendo ennyo. O Yesu, bwe ndowooza ku bbeeyi ey’amaanyi ey’Omusaayi gwo, nsanyuka olw’obunene bwakyo, kubanga akatono akamu kokka kaali kamala okulokola aboonoonyi bonna. Newankubadde ekibi kye kisibo ky’obubi n’obutakkiriza, omuwendo ogusasuliddwa ku lwaffe tegusobola kugeraageranyizibwa. Noolwekyo, buli muntu akkirize mu Kufa kwa Mukama, era ateekewo essuubi lye mu kisa kye. Katonda tajja kwegaana kisa kye eri muntu yenna. Eggulu n’ensi bisobola okukyuka, naye ekisa kya Katonda tekisobola kuggwaawo. Oh, ssanyu ki ery’amaanyi eriri mu mutima gwange nga ndowooza ku bulungi bwo obutayinza kukkirizika, O Yesu! Njagala okuleeta aboonoonyi bonna ku bigere byo basobole okugulumiza ekisa kyo mu myaka egitaggwaawo (Diary of Saint Maria Faustina Kowalska, 72). |
| You expired, Jesus, but the source of life gushed forth for souls, and the ocean of mercy opened up for the whole world. O Fount of Life, unfathomable Divine Mercy, envelop the whole world and empty Yourself out upon us. | Waweddeko, Yesu, naye ensibuko y’obulamu yasanyuka eri emyoyo, era ennyanja y’ekisa ky’okusaasira n’egulebwa eri ensi yonna. O Fount of Life, ekisa kya Katonda ekitasobola kukomekkerezebwa, gubikke ensi yonna era weefumbe ku ffe. |
| O Blood and Water, which gushed forth from the Heart of Jesus as a fount of mercy for us, I trust in You! (three times) | O Musayi n'amazzi, ebyava mu mutima gwa Yesu ng'ensulo y'ekisa gyetuli, nkukkiririzaamu! (emirundi esatu) |
| The Apostles’ Creed | Enzikiriza y'Abatume |
| I believe in God, the Father Almighty, Creator of heaven and earth, and in Jesus Christ, his only Son, our Lord, who was conceived by the power of the Holy Spirit, born of the Virgin Mary, suffered under Pontius Pilate, was crucified, died and was buried; he descended to the dead; on the third day he rose again from the dead; he ascended into Heaven, and is seated at the right hand of God the Father Almighty; from there he will come to judge the living and the dead. | Nzikiriza mu Katonda, Kitaawe Omuyinza w’ebintu byonna, Omukama owatonda eggulu n’ensi, era ne mu Yesu Kristo, omwana we omu yekka, Mukama waffe, eyazaalibwa olw’amaanyi g’Omwoyo Omutukuvu, eyazaalibwa ku Maria Omukyala atalina musajja, yabonyaabonyezebwa mu mikono gya Pontiyo Piraato, yafa era n’aziikibwa; yagenda mu bafu; ku lunaku olwokusatu yazukira mu bafu; yagenda mu ggulu, era atuula ku mukono ogwa ddyo ogwa Katonda Kitaawe Omuyinza w’ebintu byonna; okuva eyo ajja kujja okusalira abalamu n’abafu omusango. |
| I believe in the Holy Spirit, the holy Catholic Church, the Communion of Saints, the forgiveness of sins, the resurrection of the body, and life everlasting. | Nzikiriza mu Mwoyo Omutukuvu, Ekkanisa entukuvu ey'Abakatuliki, Obumu bw'abatukuvu, Okusonyiyibwa ebibi, Okuzuukira kw'omubiri, n'obulamu obutaggwawo. |
| Optional Closing Prayers | Essaala ez'okuggalawo ezitali za bulijjo |
| Eternal God, in whom mercy is endless and the treasury of compassion, inexhaustible, look kindly upon us and increase Your mercy in us, that in difficult moments we might not despair nor become despondent, but with great confidence submit ourselves to Your holy will, which is Love and Mercy itself. | Katonda ow’obulamu obutaggwaawo, mu ye ekisa kye tekiggwaawo era n’ekitabo ky’obusaasizi, ekitaggwaawo, tulabirire bulungi era oyongere ku kisa kyo mu ffe, tusobole okuba nga mu biseera ebizibu tetwewala oba okuggwaamu essuubi, wabula nga twesiga nnyo okukola by’oyagala eby’obutukuvu, eby’okwagala n’Obusaasizi bwennyini. |
| O Greatly Merciful God, Infinite Goodness, today all mankind calls out from the abyss of its misery to Your mercy, to Your compassion, O God; and it is with its mighty voice of misery that it cries out. Gracious God, do not reject the prayer of this earth’s exiles! O Lord, Goodness beyond our understanding, Who are acquainted with our misery through and through, and know that by our own power we cannot ascend to You, we implore You: anticipate us with Your grace and keep on increasing Your mercy in us, that we may faithfully do Your holy will all through our life and at death’s hour. Let the omnipotence of Your mercy shield us from the darts of our salvation’s enemies, that we may with confidence, as Your children, await Your [Son’s] final coming, that day known to You alone. And we expect to obtain everything promised us by Jesus in spite of all our wretchedness. For Jesus is our Hope: through His merciful Heart, as through an open gate, we pass through to heaven (Diary, 1570). | Katonda Omusaasizi Ennyo, Obulungi obutaggwaawo, leero abantu bonna bakaaba okuva mu kizikiza ky’obuzibu bwabwe nga basaba ekisa kyo n’okusaasira kwo, Katonda; era bakuba okuva mu ddoboozi lyabwe ery’amaanyi ery’obuzibu. Katonda ow’ekisa, togaana kusaba kw’abagoberezi b’ensi eno! O Mukama, Obulungi obusukka ku kutegeera kwaffe, Oyo amanyi obuzibu bwaffe buli kimu, era amanyi nti mu maanyi gaffe tetusobola kukulinnyaako, tukusaba: tuwe ekisa kyo era weeyongere okwongera ekisa kyo mu ffe, tusobole okukola by’oyagala eby’obutukuvu mu bulamu bwaffe bwonna ne mu kiseera ky’okufa. Leka amaanyi ag’ekisa kyo gatukuume okuva mu mivuyo gy’abalabe b’obulokozi bwaffe, tusobole okubeera n’obwesige ng’abaana bo, okulindirira okujja kwa [Omwana wo] okusembayo, olunaku olumanyiddwa ggwe wekka. Era tusuubira okufuna byonna bye twasuubizibwa Yesu newankubadde obuzibu bwaffe bwonna. Kubanga Yesu ye ssuubi lyaffe: mu mutima gwe ogw’ekisa, nga tuyita mu luggi olugazi, tuyingira mu ggulu (Diary, 1570). |
| Prayer to St. Jude | Okusaba eri St. Jude |
| Most holy Apostle, St. Jude, faithful servant and friend of Jesus, the Church honors and invokes you universally, as the patron of difficult cases, of things almost despaired of. | Omutume omutukuvu, St. Jude, omuweereza omwesigwa era mukwano gwa Yesu, Ekkanisa ekussaamu ekitiibwa n'okukusaba mu nsi yonna ng'omuwabuzi w'embeera enzibu, eziba ziweddeko essuubi. |
| Pray for me, as I feel helpless and alone. | Nsabira, kubanga mpulira nga sirina buyambi era ndi nzekka. |
| Intercede with God for me that He bring visible and speedy help where help is almost despaired of. Come to my assistance in this great need that I may receive the consolation and help of heaven in all my necessities, tribulations, and sufferings, particularly: | Nkwatira ku Katonda ansabireko asobole okuleeta obuyambi obulabika era obwangu mu kiseera nga obuyambi buli kumpi okuggwaamu essuubi. Jjangu onnyambe mu bwetaavu buno obunene nsobole okufuna okubudaabudibwa n’obuyambi okuva mu ggulu mu byonna bye nneetaaga, ebizibu n’okubonaabona kwange, ddala: |
| and that I may praise God with you and all the saints forever. I promise, O Blessed St. Jude, to be ever mindful of this great favor granted me by God and to always honor you as my special and powerful patron, and to gratefully encourage devotion to you. | Era nti nsobole okutendereza Katonda wamu nammwe n’abatukuvu bonna emirembe gyonna. Nsuubiza, Omutukuvu St. Jude Omugulumiziddwa, okubeera nga ntegeera bulijjo ekisa kino ekinene kye nnaweebwa Katonda era n’okukuwa ekitiibwa ng’omukulembeze wange ow’enjawulo era ow’amaanyi, era okusaba abantu okukwegattako mu bwesigwa. |
| Praise to the Sacred Heart | Ekitiibwa eri omutima Omutukuvu |
| May the Most Sacred Heart of Jesus be adored, and loved in all the tabernacles until the end of time. Amen. | Omutima gwa Yesu ogusinga obutukuvu guweebwe ekitiibwa era gwagalibwe mu masabo gonna okutuusa ku nkomerero y'ensi. Amiina. |
| May the most Sacred Heart of Jesus be praised and glorified now and forever. Amen. | Omutima gwa Yesu ogutukuvu ennyo gutenderezebwe era guweebwe ekitiibwa kati ne mu biseera byonna. Amiina. |
| St. Jude pray for me and hear my prayers. Amen. | St. Jude ansabire era awulire okusaba kwange. Amiina. |
| Blessings | Emikisa |
| Blessed be the Sacred Heart of Jesus. | Omutima omutukuvu ogwa Yesu gutenderezebwe. |
| Blessed be the Immaculate Heart of Mary. | Omutima gwa Maliyamu ogutalina kamogo gutenderezebwe. |
| Blessed be St. Jude Thaddeus, in all the world and for all Eternity. | Atenderezebwe St. Jude Thaddeus, mu nsi yonna ne mu biseera byonna eby'olubeerera. |
| Concluding Prayers | Okusaba okukomekkereza |
| Our Father Hail Mary Glory Be | Kitaffe Hail Mary Ekitiibwa Kibeere |
| Jesus and His 12 Apostles, over 13 consecutive days | Yesu n'Abatume be 12, mu nnaku ezisukka mu 13 ezeegattira awamu |
| Opening Prayer (Read Twice) | Okusaba okuggulawo (Soma emirundi ebiri) |
| Oh my 13 Blessed souls so wise and understanding, I ask you for the Love of God that my request be answered. | Oo, emyoyo gyange 13 egy'omukisa era egy'amagezi n'okutegeera, mbasaba okwagala kwa Katonda okusaba kwange kuddibweemu. |
| Of you I ask for the sake of the blood that Jesus shed that my request be answered. | Nkubuuza ku lw'omusaayi Yesu gwe yayiwa, okusaba kwange kuddibwemu. |
| Prayer to Our Lord | Okusaba eri Mukama waffe |
| My Lord Jesus Christ that your protection wrap me with your arms. Guard me with your eyes. O God of kindness you have been my defender in life and death. I ask that you free me from the difficulties that torment me. | Mukama wange Yesu Kristo, okukuuma kwo kunzibe mu mikono gyo. Nkuume n’amaaso go. Katonda ow’ekisa obeera omulwanirizi wange mu bulamu ne mu kufa. Nkusaba onnyambe okunzigya mu buzibu obunkubye. |
| Closing Prayer | Essaala ey'okuggalawo |
| My 13 blessed souls so wise and understanding having received the grace I seek from you (state your request) I will be devoted to you. | Emyoyo gyange 13 egy'omukisa gya magezi era nga gitegeera olw'okufuna ekisa kye njagala okuva gy'oli (tegeeza ky'oyagala) nja kubeera omwesigwa gy'oli. |
| Instruction | Okulagira |
| Say 13 Our Father's and 13 Hail Mary's for 13 consecutive days. | Saba Our Father emirundi 13 ne Hail Mary emirundi 13 mu nnaku 13 eziddiriŋŋana. |
| Prayer to Pope St. Leo the Great | Okusaba eri Paapa St. Leo ow'amaanyi |
| Glory be to thee, O Jesus, Lion of the Tribe of Judah! that hast raised up in thy Church a Lion to defend her in those dark times, when holy Faith was most exposed to danger. | Ekitiibwa kibeere gy'oli, O Yesu, Empologoma y'Ekika ky'Abayudaaya! eyagulumiza mu Kkanisa yo Empologoma okugikuuma mu biseera ebyo ebizibu, nga Okukkiriza okutukuvu kwali mu bulabe obusinga. |
| Thou charged Peter with confirming his Brethren (St. Luke, xxii. 32.); and we have seen Leo, in whom Peter lived, fulfill this office with sovereign authority. We have heard the acclamation of the holy Council, which, in admiration at the heavenly teachings of Leo, proclaimed the signal favour thou conferred on thy Flock, when thou commanded Peter to feed both Sheep and Lambs. | Ow’ekitiibwa Peter yategeezeddwa okukakasa ab’oluganda be (St. Luke, xxii. 32); era tulabye Leo, mu ye Peter yabeeramu, ng’atuukiriza omulimu guno n’obuyinza obw’enkizo. Tuwulidde okusiima kw’Olukiiko olutukuvu, olwasanyuka olw’okuyigiriza kwa Leo okuva mu ggulu, ne lutegeeza ekisa eky’amaanyi kye wawa ku kisibo kyo, bwe walagira Peter okuliisa endiga n’abaana b’endiga. |
| O holy Pontiff Leo! thou worthily represented Peter in his Chair, whence thy apostolic teaching ceased not to flow, ever beautiful in its truth and majesty. The Church of thine own day honored thee as the great Teacher of Faith; and the Church of every succeeding age has recognized thee as one of the most learned Doctors and preachers of the divine Word. | Omutukuvu Pontiff Leo! wakyoleka bulungi Peter mu kifo kye, era okuyigiriza kwo okw’obusumba tekwalekera awo kukulukuta, nga kwali kwa mazima n’obugumu obulungi. Ekkanisa ey’omulembe gwo yakussaamu ekitiibwa ng’Omuyigiriza omukulu ow’Okukkiriza; ate ekkanisa eziddako zonna zakumanyiira ng’omu ku basomesa abamanyi ennyo n’ababuulizi b’Ekigambo kya Katonda. |
| From thy throne in heaven, where now thou reign, pour forth upon us the understanding of the great Mystery which thou was called on to defend. Under thy inspired pen, this mystery grows clear; we see how sublimely it harmonizes with all other mysteries; and Faith delights at gaining so close a view of the divine object of its belief. Oh! strengthen this Faith within us. | Okuva ku ntebe yo ey’obwakabaka mu ggulu, gy’oli kati ng’ofuga, tuwe obumanyirivu bw’ekyama ekikulu ky’oyitibwa okukuuma. Wansi w’ekiwandiiko kyo eky’omwoyo, ekyaama kino kyeyongera okutegeerekeka; tulaba engeri gye kikwatagana bulungi n’ebintu ebirala byonna ebyama; era Okukkiriza kusanyuka olw’okufuna okulaba okumpi ku kigendererwa kya Katonda kye tukkirizaamu. Oba! Tugumye Okukkiriza kuno mu ffe. |
| The Incarnate Word is blasphemed in these our own times; avenge his glory, by sending us men of thy zeal and learning. | Ekigambo ekifukiriddwa kiyisibwaamu obuvvoozi mu biseera byaffe bino; okuwoolera ekitiibwa kye, nga otusindikira abantu ab'obunyiikivu n'okumanya. |
| For Our Times | Mu biseera byaffe |
| Thou triumphed over barbarian invaders: Attila acknowledged the influence of thy sanctity and eloquence, by withdrawing his troops from the Christian land they infested. | Wawangula abalumbaganyi abatamanyi: Attila yakkiriza obukulu bw'obutukuvu bwo n'okwogera kwo, ng'akomyawo amagye ge okuva mu ttaka ly'Abakristaayo lye baali balumbye. |
| In these our days, there have risen up new barbarians, civilized barbarians, who would persuade us that religion should be eliminated from Education, and that the State, in its laws and institutions, should simply ignore our Lord Jesus Christ, the King to whom all power has been given, not only in heaven but on Earth also (St. Matth. xxviii. 18). | Mu nnaku zino, waliwo abantu abapya abatalina mpisa, abakola ebintu eby’obulungi naye nga bakyali ba kitalo, abasobola okutukubiriza nti eddiini eteekwa okuggibwa mu by’enjigiriza, era nti Gavumenti mu mateeka gaayo ne mu bitongole byayo, eteekwa okuleka okussaamu ekitiibwa Mukama waffe Yesu Kristo, Kabaka eyaweebwa obuyinza bwonna mu ggulu n’ensi (St. Matth. xxviii. 18). |
| Oh! help us by thy powerful intercession, for our danger is extreme. Many are seduced, and are apostates whilst flattering themselves that they are still Christians. | Oo! tuyambe n'obuyinza bwo obw'okusaba, kubanga akabi kaffe kali waggulu nnyo. Bangi basikiriziddwa era bakyuse okuva mu mazima nga beekulisa nti bakyali Bakristaayo. |
| Pray that the light, that is left within us, may not be extinguished, and that the public scandals, which now exist, may be brought to an end. Attila was but a pagan; our modern statesmen and Governments are, or, at least, call themselves, Christians: have pity on them, and gain for them light to see the precipice to which they are hurrying society. | Saba nti omusana oguli mu ffe teguzikizibwa, era nti obulimba bw’abantu abangi obuliwo kati bukomewo. Attila yali mukkiriza wa bannansi; abakulembeze baffe ab’omulembe guno ne Gavumenti zaffe beeyita Bakristayo: babalabirire, mubafunire omusana okulaba ekifo ekibi gye batwala abantu. |
| For the Faithful and the Church | Ku lw'Abakkiriza n'Eklezia |
| These days of Paschal Time must remind thee, O holy Pontiff! of the Easters thou didst once spend, here on earth, when, surrounded by the Neophytes, thou gave them the nourishment of thy magnificent Discourses: pray for the Faithful, who have this Easter, risen to a new life with Christ. | Ennaku zino ez’Obudde bwa Paschal zirina okukujjukiza, O Pontiff omutukuvu! ku Easter gyewakolako emabega wano ku nsi, nga weebaseemu abayigirizwa abapya, ng’obawa obulamu mu bigambo byo eby’amaanyi: saba olw’Abakkiriza abali mu Easter eno, abazzeemu obulamu obupya ne Kristo. |
| What they most stand in need of is, a fuller and better knowledge of this their Saviour, in order that they may cling more closely to him, and persevere in his holy service. Thy prayers must get them this knowledge; by thy prayers, thou must teach them what he is both in his Divine and Human Nature: that, as God, He is their Last End, and their Judge after death; as Man, their Brother, their Redeemer, their Model. | Kye basinga okwetaaga kwe kumanya obulungi n’okutegeera omuwendo gwabwe Omulokozi, basobole okumwegattako ennyo era bawereze mu buweereza bwe obutukuvu. Okusaba kwo kwe kuba kuyamba okubawa okumanya kuno; okusaba kwo, olina okubayigiriza ky’ali mu mbeera ye eya Katonda n’ey’omuntu: nga Katonda, Ye ye Nkomerero yaabwe, era Omutongole waabwe oluvannyuma lw’okufa; nga Muntu, Muganda waabwe, Omulokozi waabwe, n’ekyokulabirako kyabwe. |
| Bless, O Leo! and help the Pontiff who is now thy successor on the Chair of Peter. Show now thy love for that Rome, whose sacred and eternal destinies were so frequently the subject of thy glowing and heavenly eloquence. | Bless, O Leo! era oyambe Pontiff eyali akuliddemu ku ntebe ya Peter. Laga okwagala kwo eri Rome, eyalina ebyafaayo eby'obutukuvu n'ebikadde ebyayogerwako mu bigambo byo eby'amaanyi n'eby'eggulu. |
| Intention | Ekigendererwa |
| We also ask, St Leo, that you pray for us in our intention (mention request here…) | Era tusaba, St Leo, otusabire mu kigendererwa kyaffe (yogera okusaba kwaffe wano…) |
| Pope St Leo the Great, pray for us! | Ppaapa St Leo Omukulu, tusabire! |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Guadalupe | Okusaba eri Mukyala Waffe ow'e Guadalupe |
| Remember, O most gracious Virgin of Guadalupe, that in your heavenly apparitions on the mount of Tepeyac, you promised to show your compassion and pity toward all who, loving and trusting you, seek your help and call upon you in their necessities and afflictions. | Jjukira, O Mweru Omulungi ennyo ow’e Guadalupe, nti mu kweyoleka kwo okw’omu ggulu ku lusozi Tepeyac, wasuubiza okulaga obusaasizi n’obutabangula eri abo bonna abakwagala era abakwesiga nga banoonya obuyambi bwo n’okukusaba mu byetaago ne mu buzibu bwabwe. |
| You promised to hear our supplications, to dry our tears, and to give us consolation and relief. | Watusuubiza okuwulira okusaba kwaffe, okukaza amaziga gaffe, n'okutubudaabuda n'okutuwa obuweerero. |
| Never has it been known that anyone who fled to your protection, implored your help, or sought your intercession, was left unaided. Inspired by this confidence, we fly to you, O Mary, ever-Virgin Mother of the true God! | Tekyali kumanyiddwa nti omuntu yenna eyaddukira mu bukuumi bwo, yasaba obuyambi bwo, oba okusaba okuwuliriza kwo, yalekebwa nga talina buyambi. Nga tukubirizibwa obwesige buno, tukujjira gyoli, O Mary, Maama Omweru ow’Omukama omutuufu! |
| Though grieving under the weight of our sins, we come to prostrate ourselves before you. We fully trust that, standing beneath your shadow and protection, nothing will trouble or afflict us, nor do we need to fear illness or misfortune, or any other sorrow. | Wadde nga tukyali mu bulumi olw’ebibi byaffe, tujja okukusinziza. Tukakasa nti nga tuli wansi w’ekisiikirize kyo n’obukuumi bwo, tewali kintu kyonna kijja kutulumya oba okutubonereza, era tetulina kutya bulwadde oba obuzibu bwonna, oba ennaku endala yonna. |
| O Virgin of Guadalupe, you want to remain with us through your admirable Image, you who are our Mother, our health, and our life. | O Virgin wa Guadalupe, oyagala okusigala naffe ng'oyita mu kifaananyi kyo ekirungi ennyo, ggwe omukazi waffe, obulamu bwaffe era obulamu bwaffe. |
| Placing ourselves beneath your maternal gaze, and having recourse to you in all our necessities, we need do nothing more. | Bwe tweteeka wansi w'okutunuulirwa kwo, era nga tukusaba mu byetaago byaffe byonna, tetwetaaga kukola kintu kirala kyonna. |
| O Holy Mother of God, despise not our petitions, but in your mercy hear and answer us. Amen. | O Mukama Katonda, togaana kusaba kwaffe, naye mu kisa kyo tuwulire era otuddemu. Amiina. |
| Dearest Lady of Guadalupe, fruitful Mother of holiness, teach me your ways of gentleness and strength. Hear my humble prayer offered with heartfelt confidence to beg this favor. | Mukyala omwagalwa owa Guadalupe, Maama ow'obutukuvu era omuzadde w'ebibala, njigiriza engeri zo ez'obuwombeefu n'amaanyi. Wulira okusaba kwange okw'obuwombeefu okuweereddwa n'obwesige obuva mu mutima okusaba ekisa kino. |
| Prayer to the Holy Name | Okusaba eri erinnya ettukuvu |
| O merciful Jesus! Who didst in Thine early infancy commence Thine office of Savior, by shedding Thy precious Blood and assuming for us that Name which is above all names; we thank Thee for such early proofs of Thine infinite love. | Yesu omusaasizi! Ani eyatandika okukola emirimu gyo ng’omuwonyi mu buto bwo, ng’afuka omusaayi gwo ogw’omuwendo era n’atuwa erinnya eryo erisinga amannya gonna; tukwebaza olw’obujulizi obwo obw’okwagala kwo okutaggwaawo. |
| We venerate Thy sacred Name, in union with the profound respect of the Angel who first announced it to the earth, and unite our affections to the sentiments of tender devotion which the adorable Name of Jesus has in all ages enkindled in the hearts of Thy servants. | Tukwasa erinnya lyo ettukuvu, mu kwegatta n'okussa ekitiibwa ku malayika eyasooka okulirangirira ku nsi, era tukwataganya okwagala kwaffe n'obwesigwa obulungi obw'erinnya lya Yesu ery'ekitiibwa mu biseera byonna nga bw'ateeka mu mitima gy'abaweereza bo. |
| Animated with a firm faith in Thine unerring word, and penetrated with confidence in Thy mercy, we now most humbly remind Thee of the promise Thou hast made, that when two or three should gather in Thy Name, Thou Thyself wouldst be in the midst of them. | Nga tukkiriza nnyo ekigambo kyo ekituufu, era nga twesiga obusaasizi bwo, kati tukusaba mu buwombeefu okujjukira ekisuubizo ky’oyogeddeko nti singa abantu babiri oba basatu bakuŋŋaanira mu linnya lyo, ggwe mwennyini ojja kubeera wakati waabwe. |
| Come, then, into the midst of us, most amiable Jesus! | Jjangu, kati mu makkati gaffe, Yesu omwagalwa ennyo! |
| For it is in Thy Sacred Name we are here assembled. | Kubanga mu linnya lyo ettukuvu mwe twakuŋŋaanira. |
| Come into our hearts, that Thy Holy Spirit may pray in and by us; and mercifully grant us, through that adorable Name, which is the joy of heaven, the terror of hell, the consolation of the afflicted, and the solid ground of our unlimited confidence, all the petitions we make in this Novena. | Jjangu mu mitima gyaffe, Omwoyo Wo Omutukuvu asabe mu ffe era ku lwaffe; era otuwa ekisa okuyita mu linnya eryo ery'ekitiibwa, eriri essanyu ly'eggulu, obutya bw'omuliro ogw'omu hell, okubudaabudibwa kw'abo abali mu bulumi, n'obusobozi obunywevu obw'okwesiga kwaffe okutalina kkomo, ebisaba byonna bye tukola mu Novena eno. |
| O Blessed Mother of our Redeemer; who didst participate so deeply in the sufferings of thy dear Son, when He shed His sacred Blood, and assumed for us the Name of Jesus; obtain for us through that adorable Name, the favors we petition in this Novena. | O Mukama Omukazi Omutukuvu ow’Omulezi waffe; eyakola nnyo mu kubonaabona kwa Mwana wo omwagalwa, bwe yamala okufuka omusaayi gwe omutukuvu, era n’atutwalira erinnya lya Yesu; otusabireko mu linnya eryo erisanyusa, tusobole okufuna obulungi bwe tusaba mu Novena eno. |
| Beg, also, that the most ardent love may imprint on our hearts that sacred Name, that it may be always in our minds, and frequently on our lips; that it may be our defence in temptations, and our refuge in danger, during our lives, and our consolation and support in the hour of death. | Era tusaba nti okwagala okw’amaanyi kuteeke erinnya eryo ettukuvu mu mitima gyaffe, lubeere mu birowoozo byaffe bulijjo, era ku mimwa gyaffe emirundi mingi; lubeere obukuumi bwaffe mu kukemebwa, n’obuddukiro bwaffe mu kabi, mu bulamu bwaffe, era lubeere okubudaabuda n’okuyamba mu kiseera ky’okufa. |
| Litany of the Holy Name | Olukalala lw'Erinnya Eritukuvu |
| V. Lord, have mercy on us. R. Christ, have mercy on us. | V. Mukama, tulaga ekisa. R. Kristo, tulaga ekisa. |
| V. Lord, have mercy on us. Jesus, hear us. R. Jesus, graciously hear us. | V. Mukama, twasaasire. Yesu, tuwulirize. R. Yesu, mu kisa otuwulirize. |
| V. God the Father of Heaven, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Katonda Kitaffe ow'omu ggulu, R. Otusaasire. |
| V. God the Son, Redeemer of the world, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Katonda Omwana, Omununula w'ensi, R. Otusaasire. |
| V. God the Holy Spirit, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Katonda Omwoyo Omutukuvu, R. Otusaasire. |
| V. Holy Trinity, one God, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Obusatu obutukuvu, Katonda omu, R. Tusaasire. |
| V. Jesus, Son of the living God, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Yesu, Omwana wa Katonda omulamu, R. Otusaasire. |
| After each line, answer: have mercy on us. | Buli luvannyuma lw'olunyiriri, yogerako nti: tusaasire. |
| Jesus, splendor of the Father. Jesus, brightness of eternal light. Jesus, King of glory. Jesus, sun of justice. Jesus, Son of the Virgin Mary. Jesus, most amiable. Jesus, most admirable. Jesus, the mighty God. Jesus, Father of the world to come. Jesus, angel of great counsel. Jesus, most powerful. Jesus, most patient. Jesus, most obedient. Jesus, meek and humble of heart. Jesus, lover of chastity. Jesus, lover of us. Jesus, God of peace. Jesus, author of life. Jesus, example of virtues. Jesus, zealous lover of souls. Jesus, our God. Jesus, our refuge. Jesus, father of the poor. Jesus, treasure of the faithful. Jesus, good Shepherd. Jesus, true light. Jesus, eternal wisdom. Jesus, infinite goodness. Jesus, our way and our life. Jesus, joy of Angels. Jesus, King of the Patriarchs. Jesus, Master of the Apostles. Jesus, teacher of the Evangelists. Jesus, strength of Martyrs. Jesus, light of Confessors. Jesus, purity of Virgins. Jesus, crown of Saints. | Yesu, ekitiibwa kya Kitaffe. Yesu, omusana gw'ekitangaala eky'enkomeredde. Yesu, Kabaka w'ekitiibwa. Yesu, enjuba y'obwenkanya. Yesu, Omwana wa Namukadde Maryamu. Yesu, asinga okwagalwa. Yesu, asinga okusiimibwa. Yesu, Katonda ow'amaanyi. Yesu, Kitaawe w'ensi ejja. Yesu, malayika ow'amagezi amangi. Yesu, asinga amaanyi. Yesu, asinga obugumiikiriza. Yesu, asinga okuwulira. Yesu, omuwombeefu era omwetoowaze mu mutima. Yesu, ayagala obutukuvu. Yesu, ayagala ffe. Yesu, Katonda ow'emirembe. Yesu, eyatandikawo obulamu. Yesu, ekyokulabirako ky'obulungi. Yesu, omwagalwa w'emyoyo ow'obunyiikivu. Yesu, Katonda waffe. Yesu, ekiddukiro kyaffe. Yesu, kitaawe w'abaavu. Yesu, eky'obugagga ky'abeesigwa. Yesu, Omusumba omulungi. Yesu, ekitangaala eky'amazima. Yesu, amagezi agataliiko kkomo. Yesu, obulungi obutaliiko kkomo. Yesu, ekkubo lyaffe n'obulamu bwaffe. Yesu, essanyu lya bamalayika. Yesu, Kabaka w'Abakadde. Yesu, Mukulu w'Abatume. Yesu, omuyigiriza w'Ababuulizi b'enjiri. Yesu, amaanyi g'Abajulizi. Yesu, ekitangaala ky'Abakkiriza. Yesu, obutukuvu bw'Abawala Abembeerera. Yesu, engule y'Abatukuvu. |
| V. Be merciful, R. spare us, O Jesus. | V. Beera musaasizi, R. Otusonyiwe, O Yesu. |
| V. Be merciful, R. graciously hear us, O Jesus. | V. Beera musaasizi, R. Tuwulirize n'ekisa, O Yesu. |
| V. From all evil, R. deliver us, O Jesus. | V. Okuva mu bubi bwonna, R. otuwonye, O Yesu. |
| After each line, answer: deliver us, O Jesus. | Buli lwaanyuma lw'olunyiriri, zzaamu: otuleete, O Yesu. |
| From all sin. From Your wrath. From the snares of the devil. From the spirit of fornication. From everlasting death. From the neglect of Your inspirations. By the mystery of Your holy Incarnation. By Your Nativity. By Your Infancy. By Your most divine Life. By Your labors. By Your agony and passion. By Your cross and dereliction. By Your sufferings. By Your death and burial. By Your Resurrection. By Your Ascension. By Your institution of the most Holy Eucharist. By Your joys. By Your glory. | Okuva mu kibi kyonna. Okuva mu busungu bwo. Okuva mu mitego gya dayimooni. Okuva mu mwoyo gw’obwenzi. Okuva mu kufa okutaggwaawo. Okuva mu kuleka obulagirizi bwo. Mu kyama ky’okufuuka omuntu kwo okutukuvu. Mu kuzaalibwa kwo. Mu buto bwo. Mu bulamu bwo obw’ekitiibwa ennyo. Mu mirimu gyo. Mu kubonaabona n’okunyiiga kwo. Mu musaalaba gwo n’okulekera awo okukola. Mu kubonaabona kwo. Mu kufa kwo n’okuziikibwa kwo. Mu kuzuukira kwo. Mu kukyusibwa kwo okugenda mu ggulu. Mu kutandika kw’Ekirabo Ekisinga Obutukuvu Eky’Emmere Enjereere. Mu ssanyu lyo. Mu kitiibwa kyo. |
| V. Lamb of God, who takest away the sins of the world, R. spare us, O Jesus. | V. Omwana wa Katonda, atwala ebibi by'ensi, R. Otusonyiwe, O Yesu. |
| V. Lamb of God, who takest away the sins of the world, R. graciously hear us, O Jesus. | V. Omwana wa Katonda, atwala ebibi by'ensi, R. O Yesu, tuwulire n'ekisa. |
| V. Lamb of God, who takest away the sins of the world, R. have mercy on us, O Jesus. | V. Lamb wa Katonda, aggyawo ebibi by'ensi, R. otusaasire, Ai Yesu. |
| V. Jesus, hear us. R. Jesus, graciously hear us. | V. Yesu, tuwulire. R. Yesu, mu kisa otuwulire. |
| O Lord Jesus Christ, Thou hast said, Ask and you shall receive, seek, and you shall find, knock, and it shall be opened to you. | Mukama waffe Yesu Kristo, wagamba nti, Saba muliweebwa, noonya mulizuula, konkona muliggulirwawo. |
| Grant, we beg Thee, to us who ask it, the gift of Thy most divine love, that we may ever love Thee with our whole heart, in word and deed, and never cease praising Thee. | Grant, tukusaba ggwe, ffe abasaba ekirabo ky'okwagala kwo okutukuvu ennyo, tusobole okukwagala n'omutima gwaffe gwonna mu bigambo ne mu bikolwa, era tetulekera awo kukutendereza. |
| Give us, O Lord, as much a lasting fear as a lasting love of Thy Holy Name, for Thee, who live and are King for ever and ever, never fail to govern those whom Thou have solidly established in Thy love. | Tuwe, O Mukama, okutya okw’obulamu obutaggwaawo nga bwe kuli okwagala okw’obulamu obutaggwaawo eri Erinnya Lyo Eritukuvu, kubanga Ggwe, omutuufu era Kabaka ow’emirembe gyonna, tolemererwa kulabirira abo b’otadde mu kwagala kwo. |
| Mother of Holy Hope | Maama w'Essuubi Ery'Omutukuvu |
| I am the mother of fair love, and of fear, and of knowledge, and of holy hope. In me is all grace of the way and of the truth; in me is all hope of life and of virtue. Come to me all that desire me and be filled with my fruits (Sirach 24:24-26). | Nze nze maama w’okwagala okutukuvu, era ow’entiisa, era ow’amagezi, era ow’essuubi ettukuvu. Mu nze mulimu ekisa kyonna eky’ekkubo n’amazima; mu nze mulimu essuubi lyonna ery’obulamu n’obulungi. Jjangu gyendi bonna abankwana mujjuzibwe ebibala byange (Sirach 24:24-26). |
| O Blessed Virgin Mary, Mother of Grace, Hope of the world. Hear us, your children, who cry to you. | O Mbeerera Omukama Maria, Maama w'ekisa, essuubi ly'ensi. Tuwulire, abaana bo abakukaabira. |
| O God, who by the marvelous protection of the Blessed Virgin Mary has strengthened us firmly in hope, grant we beseech You, that by persevering in prayer at her admonition, we may obtain the favors we devoutly implore. Through Christ Our Lord. | Ai Katonda, eyayita mu bukuumi obw’ekitalo bw’omukyala omutukuvu Maria n’atunyweza mu ssuubi, twakusaba otuyambe, nga tuyimirira mu kusaba nga tumugoberera, tufune ebyo bye tusaba n’obwesigwa. Mu Kristo Mukama waffe. |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Hope | Okusaba eri Mukyala waffe ow'Essuubi |
| O Mary, my Mother, I kneel before you with heavy heart. The burden of my sins oppresses me. The knowledge of my weakness discourages me. I am beset by fears and temptations of every sort. Yet I am so attached to the things of this world that instead of longing for Heaven I am filled with dread at the thought of death. | O Mary, Maama wange, Nkwatira mu maaso go n’omutima oguzito. Obuzibu bw’ebibi byange bunyigiriza. Okumanya obunafu bwange kunnyiga amaanyi. Ndi mu kutya n’okukemebwa okw’enjawulo zonna. Naye ndi mu kwagala ebintu eby’ensi eno nga mu kifo ky’okwegomba eggulu njijjiddwa okutya olw’okufa. |
| O Mother of Mercy, have pity on me in my distress. You are all-powerful with your Divine Son. He can refuse no request of your Immaculate Heart. Show yourself a true Mother to me by being my advocate before His throne. O Refuge of Sinners and Hope of the Hopeless, to whom shall I turn if not you? | O Maama w’Obulungi, nkusaasire mu buzibu bwange. Oli wa maanyi gonna n’Omwana wo ow’obwakatonda. Tayinza kugaana kusaba kwonna okuva ku mutima gwo ogutukuvu. Nnyonnyola nga Maama ow’amazima gyendi ng’oba omuwolereza wange mu maaso g’entebe ye. O Kifo ky’Abonoonyi n’Essuubi ly’abatalina ssuubi, ani gwe nzija okuddamu singa si ggwe? |
| Obtain for me, then, O Mother of Hope, the grace of true sorrow for my sins, the gift of perfect resignation to God’s Holy Will, and the courage to take up my cross and follow Jesus. Beg of His Sacred Heart the special favor that I ask in this novena. | Nnyamba, O Maama w’Essuubi, ompe ekisa ky’okwennyika okutuufu olw’ebibi byange, ekirabo eky’okukkiriza obulungi mu kigendererwa kya Katonda Ekitukuvu, n’obuvumu okutwala omusalaba gwange n’okugoberera Yesu. Saba ku Mutima Gwe Omutukuvu ekisa eky’enjawulo kye nsaba mu novena eno. |
| But above all I pray, O dearest Mother, that through your most powerful intercession my heart may be filled with Holy Hope, so that in life’s darkest hour I may never fail to trust in God my Saviour, but by walking in the way of His commandments I may merit to be united with Him, and with you in the eternal joys of Heaven. | Naye okusinga byonna nsaba, O maama wange omwagalwa, nti ng’okuyita mu kwegayirira kwo okw’amaanyi ennyo, omutima gwange gubeere n’Essuubi Ery’Obutukuvu, kale mu kiseera ekizibu ennyo mu bulamu, nsobole obutalemererwa kwesiga Katonda Omulokozi wange, naye nga ntambulira mu makubo g’amateeka Ge, nsobole okufuna omukisa okubeera awamu Naye, nawe mu ssanyu ery’emirembe gyonna mu ggulu. |
| Mary, our Hope, have pity on us. | Mary, essuubi lyaffe, atulumirirwa. |
| Hope of the Hopeless, pray for us. | Essuubi ly'abo abatalina ssuubi, tusabire. |
| Pray this prayer once a day for 30 days | Saba essaala eno omulundi gumu buli lunaku okumala ennaku 30 |
| Pray the same prayer each day, for 30 days in a row. | Saba essaala y'emu buli lunaku, okumala ennaku 30 eziddiringana. |
| Prayer to St. Joseph | Okusaba eri St. Joseph |
| Ever blessed and glorious Joseph, kind and loving father, and helpful friend of all in sorrow! You are the good father and protector of orphans, the defender of the defenseless, the patron of those in need and sorrow. Look kindly on my request. My sins have drawn down on me the just displeasure of my God, and so I am surrounded with unhappiness. To you, loving guardian of the Family of Nazareth, do I go for help and protection. | Oli omusanyufu era ow'ekitiibwa Joseph, kitaawe omulungi era omwagalwa, era mukwano gw'abo bonna abali mu nnaku! Oli kitaawe omulungi era omukuumi w'abaana abatalina bazadde, omukuuma abatalina buyambi, omuwagizi w'abo abali mu bwetaavu n'ennaku. Laba ku kusaba kwange n'obulungi. Ebibi byange binzizaako obutali bulungi bwa Katonda wange, era kati ndi mu nnaku ennyingi. Gyoli, omukuumi omwagalwa w'Ekika ky'e Nazareth, njija gyoli okufuna obuyambi n'okukuuma. |
| Listen, then, I beg you, with fatherly concern, to my earnest prayers, and obtain for me the favors I ask. | Wulira, kale, nkwegayiridde, n'okufaayo kwa kitaawe, eri okusaba kwange okw'amazima, onfunire ebirungi bye nsaba. |
| I Ask It | Nkibuuza |
| I ask it by the infinite mercy of the eternal Son of God, which moved Him to take our nature and to be born into this world of sorrow. | Nkibuuza mu kisa ekitaggwaawo eky'Omwana wa Katonda ow'emirembe gyonna, eyakubirizibwa okutwala obulamu bwaffe n'okuzaalibwa mu nsi eno ey'ennaku. |
| I ask it by the weariness and suffering you endured when you found no shelter at the inn of Bethlehem for the holy Virgin, nor a house where the Son of God could be born. Then, being everywhere refused, you had to allow the Queen of Heaven to give birth to the world’s Redeemer in a cave. | Nkibuuza ku buggya n’okubonaabona kwe mwagumira nga temulina we musula mu kisulo ky’e Betelehemu eri Omukyala omutukuvu, oba ennyumba omwana wa Katonda gy’ayinza okuzaalirwa. Oluvannyuma ng’abantu bonna bamugaana, wateekwa okukkiriza Kabaka w’eggulu okuzaala Omulokozi w’ensi mu mpuku. |
| I ask it by the loveliness and power of that sacred Name, Jesus, which you conferred on the adorable infant. | Nkibuuza mu bulungi n'amaanyi g'erinnya eryo ettukuvu, Yesu, lye mwawa omwana oyo omuto. |
| I ask it by that painful torture you felt at the prophecy of holy Simeon, which declared the Child Jesus and His holy Mother future victims of our sins and of their great love for us. | Nkibuuza nga nkozesa obulumi bw'okutulugunyizibwa kwe wawulira mu bunnabbi bwa Simeoni omutukuvu, eyalangirira omwana Yesu ne Maama we omutukuvu ng'abanaaba aboonoonyi baffe era olw'okutwagala ennyo. |
| I ask it through your sorrow and pain of soul when the angel declared to you that the life of the Child Jesus was sought by His enemies. From their evil plan you had to flee with Him and His Blessed Mother to Egypt. I ask it by all the suffering, weariness, and labors of that long and dangerous journey. | Nkibuuza mu nnaku n’obulumi bw’omutima gwo bwe malayika yakulangirira nti obulamu bw’Omwana Yesu bwali buvumbuddwa abalabe be. Okuva mu kutegeka kwabwe okubi, walina okudduka naye ne Nnyina Omukisa okugenda e Misiri. Nkibuuza mu bulumi bwonna, okukoowa n’okufuba ku lugendo olwo olw’amaanyi era olw’akabi. |
| I ask it by all your care to protect the Sacred Child and His Immaculate Mother during your second journey, when you were ordered to return to your own country. I ask it by your peaceful life in Nazareth where you met with so many joys and sorrows. | Nkikusaba mu buvunaanyizibwa bwo bwonna okukuuma Omwana Omutukuvu n’Maama we omutukuvu mu lugendo lwo olw’okubiri, bwe wategekebwa okuddayo mu ggwanga lyo. Nkikusaba mu bulamu bwo obw’emirembe e Nazareth gye wasanga essanyu n’obulumi bungi. |
| I ask it by your great distress when the adorable Child was lost to you and His Mother for three days. I ask it by your joy at finding Him in the Temple, and by the comfort you found at Nazareth, while living in the company of the Child Jesus. I ask it by the wonderful submission He showed in His obedience to you. | Nkikubuuza mu kusaasira kwammwe okungi bwe mwafiirwa Omwana omulungi era nnyina we n’abula ennaku ssatu. Nkikubuuza mu ssanyu lyammwe ery’okumuzuula mu Yeekaalu, ne mu buwanguzi bw’omukisa gwe mwafuna e Nazareth nga mubeera wamu n’Omwana Yesu. Nkikubuuza mu kusaba okw’enjawulo kwe yalaga ng’akugondera. |
| I ask it by the perfect love and conformity you showed in accepting the Divine order to depart from this life, and from the company of Jesus and Mary. I ask it by the joy which filled your soul, when the Redeemer of the world, triumphant over death and hell, entered into the possession of His kingdom and led you into it with special honors. | Nkibuuza mu kwagala okutuufu n’okukkiriza kwe walaga ng’okkiriza ekiragiro kya Katonda okuva mu bulamu buno, n’okuva mu mukwano gwa Yesu ne Mariya. Nkibuuza mu ssanyu eryajjuza omutima gwo, nga Omulokozi w’ensi, ng’awangudde okufa n’omuliro ogutazikira, yayingira mu bwakabaka bwe era n’akuleeta mu bwo n’ekitiibwa eky’enjawulo. |
| I ask it through Mary’s glorious Assumption, and through that endless happiness you have with her in the presence of God. | Nkibuuza mu kugulumizibwa kwa Mary, n'essanyu eryo eritaggwawo ly'olina naye ng'oli ne Katonda. |
| O good father! I beg you, by all your sufferings, sorrows, and joys, to hear me and obtain for me what I ask. | O taata omulungi! Nkwegayirira, mu kubonaabona kwo kwonna, ennaku zo n'essanyu lyo, ompulire era onfunire ekyo kye nsaba. |
| Obtain for all those who have asked my prayers everything that is useful to them in the plan of God. Finally, my dear patron and father, be with me and all who are dear to me in our last moments, that we may eternally sing the praises of Jesus, Mary and Joseph. | Funa eri bonna abasabye okusaba kwange buli kimu ekibayamba mu nteekateeka ya Katonda. Ku nkomerero, mukwano gwange era kitaawe wange, beera nange n’abo bonna abankwatako mu kiseera kyaffe eky’enkomerero, tusobole okuyimba obulamu bwonna ekitiibwa kya Yesu, Mary ne Joseph. |
| O God, who said let the little children come to Me and who love children, please answer the prayers I ask in this novena through the saints’ intercession, especially | Ai Katonda, ani eyagamba abaana abato bajje gyendi era ayagala abaana, nsaba onnyambe okuddamu essaala ze nsaba mu novena eno ng’oyita mu kusabira kwa bannaddiini abatuukirivu, naddala |
| Prayer to Our Blessed Mother | Okusaba eri Maama waffe Omutukuvu |
| Most Holy Mother, hear the cries of my heart right now. Blessed Virgin, I want to be fruitful, but I have not yet been blessed with fertility. Grant me fertility over my life and end my suffering. | Maama Omutukuvu, wulira okukaaba kw'omutima gwange kati. Omukyala omugole omutukuvu, njagala okuba n'ebibala, naye sinnaweebwa mukisa gwa kuzaala. Nnyamba onnyambe okufuna obusobozi bw'okuzaala mu bulamu bwange era onzikize okubonaabona kwange. |
| Help make me a fruitful vine within my house. My children will be like olive shoots around my table. Mother of Mercy, I know I was not created so that I would be fruitless, but your Son created me to be effective in all that I do. I know that my season of fertility will come according to your Son’s timing. Amen. | Munnyambe onkoze omuzabbibu ogw’amaanyi mu nnyumba yange. Abaana bange banaaba nga amatabi g’omuyembe okwetooloola emmeeza yange. Maama w’ekisa, mmanyi nti saakolebwa kubeera atalina bibala, naye Omwana wo yankola okubeera ow’amaanyi mu byonna bye nkola. Mmanyi nti ekiseera kyange eky’okubala kinaajja ng’okusinzira ku kiseera ky’Omwana wo. Amiina. |
| Prayer to St. Gerard Majella | Okusaba eri St. Gerard Majella |
| O good St Gerard, powerful intercessor before God and wonder worker of our day, confidently I call upon you and seek your aid. | Omutukuvu Gerard omulungi, omusabirizi ow'amaanyi mu maaso ga Katonda era omukozi w'ebyewuunyo mu kiseera kyaffe, nkwesiga era nkusaba obuyambi bwo. |
| You always fulfilled God’s designs, help me now to do the holy will of God. Implore the Master of Life, from whom all parenthood proceeds, to render me fruitful in offspring, that I may raise up children to God in this life, and in the world to come, heirs to the Kingdom of His Glory. Amen. | Buli kiseera wawanga obulamu bw’ekigendererwa kya Katonda, nnyamba kati okukola ebyo byonna Katonda by’ayagala. Saba Mukama w’Obulamu, eyavaamu abazadde bonna, ampe abaana bangi nsobole okukuza abaana eri Katonda mu bulamu buno ne mu nsi ejja, nga bali basika ba Bwakabaka bwe Obugulumivu. Amiina. |
| Prayer to Sts. Joachim and Anne | Okusaba eri abatukuvu. Joachim ne Anne |
| Most Holy Saints Anne and Joachim, come close to us and guide our hand and our hearts in the way of virtue. | Abatukuvu abasinga obutukuvu Anne ne Yokaamu, mujje okumpi naffe mutukuume mu mikono gyaffe ne mu mitima gyaffe mu kkubo ery'obulungi. |
| Help us to conceive a child so that we may parent them with a gentle heart as you did. | Tuyambe okufuna omwana tusobole okumukuza n'omutima omulungi nga bwe wakola. |
| Obtain from God the grace to overcome all infertility so that we may raise our children in faith and bring our families into the Light of Jesus. | Tufune okuva eri Katonda ekisa eky'okuvvuunuka obutazaala tusobole okukuza abaana baffe mu kukkiriza era tuzze amaka gaffe mu kitangaala kya Yesu. |
| Sts Anne and Joachim, we place our petitions in your hands and ask that you lay them before the child Jesus. Amen. | Sts Anne ne Joachim, tukuteeka okusaba kwaffe mu mikono gyo era tusaba nti okukuteeke mu maaso ga Yesu omwana. Amiina. |
| Prayer to St. Colette | Okusaba eri St. Colette |
| O glorious St. Colette, I beseech you through the burning love which inflamed your heart for Jesus in the Most Blessed Sacrament, through the marvelous graces with which He enriched you, and through the loving compassion you did bear to Him in His bitter sufferings, obtain from Him for me my request for fertility which I hope to receive through your powerful intercession. Amen. | Omutukuvu St. Colette omulungi, nkusaba okuyita mu kwagala okw’amaanyi okwakuma omutima gwo eri Yesu mu Sakramentu Ekitukuvu Ennyo, okuyita mu mikisa egy’ekitalo gy’awaddeyo, n’okuyita mu kisa eky’amaanyi ky’owaddeyo mu kubonaabona kwe, onfunire okuva gy’ali okusaba kwange okw’obuzadde kye nsuubira okufuna okuyita mu kusaba kwo okw’amaanyi. Amiina. |
| Prayer to St. Raphael to Heal Infertility | Okusaba kwa St. Raphael okuwonya obutazaala |
| Glorious Archangel St. Raphael, great prince of the heavenly court, you are illustrious for your gifts of wisdom and grace. | Malayika omukulu omutukuvu St. Raphael, omulangira omukulu ow'omu luggya olw'eggulu, oli wa kitiibwa olw'ebirabo byo eby'amagezi n'ekisa. |
| You are a guide of those who journey by land or sea or air, consoler of the afflicted, and refuge of sinners. I beg you, assist me in all my needs and in all the sufferings of this life, as once you helped the young Tobias on his travels. | Oli mugobererezi w’abo abakola olugendo ku ttaka, ku nnyanja oba mu bbanga, omusanyusa abalina ennaku, era ekifo ky’okusabirako aboonoonyi. Nkusaba onnyambe mu byetaago byange byonna ne mu bulumi bwonna obw’obulamu buno, nga bwe wayamba Tobias omuto mu lugendo lwe. |
| Because you are the Medicine of God, I humbly pray you to heal the many infirmities of my soul and the ills that afflict my body, especially of infertility. | Olw'okuba ggwe ddagala lya Katonda, nkusaba mu buwombeefu okuwonya obulwadde bungi obuli mu mwoyo gwange n'obulwadde obukosa omubiri gwange, nga nnyo nnyo obutazaala. |
| I also ask for the great grace of purity to prepare me to be the temple of the Holy Spirit. | Era nsaba ekisa eky'amaanyi eky'obulongoofu okunteekateeka okubeera yeekaalu y'Omwoyo Omutukuvu. |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Fatima | Okusaba eri Our Lady of Fatima |
| Most Holy Virgin, who deigned to come to Fatima to reveal to the three little shepherds the treasures of graces hidden in the recitation of the Rosary, inspire our hearts with a sincere love of this devotion, so that by meditating on the mysteries of our redemption that are recalled in it, we may gather the fruits and obtain the conversion of sinners, the conversion of Russia and this favor which I so earnestly seek, | Omutukuvu Omusajja Omutukuvu, eyakkiriza okujja e Fatima okulaga abasumba abato basatu obugagga bw’obulungi obwekutte mu kusoma Rosary, atukubirize emitima gyaffe n’okwagala okutuufu ku kwegatta kuno, tusobole okuwandiika ku byama by’okununulwa kwaffe ebisangibwa mu kusoma kuno, tusobole okufuna ebibala n’okukyusa aboonoonyi, okukyusa Russia n’ekisa kino kye nsaba nnyo |
| which I ask of thee in this novena, for the greater Glory of God, for thine own honor and for the good of all people. | Kye nkusaba mu novena eno, olw'ekitiibwa kya Katonda ekisinga obukulu, olw'ekitiibwa kyo n'olw'obulungi bw'abantu bonna. |
| Prayer of Reparation | Essaala y'Okutabagana |
| My God, I believe, I adore, I hope and I love You! I ask pardon of You for those who do not believe, do not adore, do not hope and do not love You. Most Holy Trinity, Father, Son and Holy Spirit, I adore You profoundly, and I offer You the most precious Body, Blood, Soul and Divinity of Jesus Christ, present in all the tabernacles of the world, in reparation for the outrages, sacrileges and indifference with which He Himself is offended. And, through the infinite merits of His most Sacred Heart, and the Immaculate Heart of Mary, I beg of You the conversion of poor sinners. | Katonda wange, nzikiriza, nkwagala, nsuubira era nkukwatako! Nsaba okusonyiyibwa gy’oli ku abo abatannakkiriza, abatannakwagala, abatannasuubira era abatannakwagala. Omutukuvu Katonda Omusatu, Kitaffe, Omwana ne Mwoyo Mutukuvu, nkukwatako nnyo, era nkuwa omubiri ogusinga obulungi, omusaayi, emmeeme n’obutukuvu bwa Yesu Kristo, ali mu masinzizo gonna ag’ensi, ng’asasula olw’okukola ebibi, okunyooma n’obutafaayo kwe yennyini akwatiddwa. Era, okuyita mu bulungi obutaggwaawo bw’Omutima gwe Omutukuvu ennyo, n’Omutima omutukuvu ogwa Maryamu, nkusaba okukyusa aboonoona abatalina kyekikulu. |
| Our Father, Hail Mary Glory Be (three times each) | Kitaffe, Mukama Mariya Ekitiibwa kibeere gyoli (emirundi esatu buli kimu) |
| Prayer to St. Dominic | Okusaba eri St. Dominic |
| O renowned champion of the faith of Christ, most holy saint Dominic, who didst renounce the honour and dignity of an earthly principality to embrace the poor, laborious, and mortified life which should distinguish a disciple of Him Who has said: If any man will come after Me, let him take up his cross and follow Me. | Omuvubuka omumanyiddwa ennyo mu kukkiriza kwa Kristu, omutukuvu Dominic omutukuvu ennyo, eyaleka ekitiibwa n’obugagga bw’ensi okusanyusa abavu, abakozi, era abawulirizibwa obulumi obw’okutambulira mu bulamu obulina okwawula omuyigirizwa we Oyo agamba nti: Omuntu yenna bw’aba ayagala okunkolera emabega, amuteekeko omusalaba gwe angoberere. |
| O burning torch, who being thyself consumed with the fire of divine love, didst incessantly labour to enkindle that sacred flame in the hearts of others, look down upon me from that throne of glory where thou enjoyest the reward of all thy labours, and obtain that some sparks of that blessed fire may be lighted in my soul, to animate and encourage me under any crosses or trials with which it shall please the divine goodness to visit me. | O torch eyaka, ggwe nga wekka okyayokebwa omuliro gw’okwagala kwa Katonda, wakola bulijjo okukuma omuliro ogutukuvu mu mitima gy’abalala, nnyonera wansi okuva ku kiti ky’obugagga gy’ofunira empeera y’emirimu gyo gyonna, onfunireko obusannyalazo bw’omuliro ogwo ogw’ekisa bubeere mu mwoyo gwange, okunyweza n’okunsanyusa mu buvunaanyizibwa oba ebigezo byonna Katonda by’anaakiriza okunkuba. |
| Thou, O great Saint, regardest as nothing all the afflictions thou didst endure, and all the toils thou didst undergo for the promotion of God’s holy cause; obtain, I beseech Thee, that the same ardent love which strengthened thee, may make sweet to me labours, humiliations, disgraces, or whatever other mortifications I may have to suffer for the Name of Jesus. | Gwe, Omutukuvu omukulu, tolowooza ku buzibu bwonna bwe wawulira oba emirimu gyonna gye wakola okutumbula ekigendererwa kya Katonda ekitukuvu; nkwegayiridde, nfuna okwagala okwo okw’amaanyi okwakusobozesa, kunnyumire emirimu, okuswazibwa, okuswaza, oba ebintu ebirala byonna bye nnaasobola okufiirwa olw’erinnya lya Yesu. |
| May I ever bear in mind that nothing can happen to me but by the particular dispensation of a God Who is infinitely wise, and therefore knows what is best for my welfare; infinitely powerful, and consequently able to effect it; and above all, infinitely merciful and loving, Who has laid down His life for my redemption, and continues daily to give new proofs of His love and bounty. | Nnyinza okujjukira nti tewali kintu kyonna kisobola kunzizaako wabula nga Katonda ow’amagezi agatagambika, era amanyi ekisinga obulungi eri obulamu bwange; ow’amaanyi agatagambika, era asobola okukikola; ate okusinga byonna, ow’ekisa ekitagambika n’okwagala, eyawaayo obulamu bwe ku lwange, era akiraga buli lunaku mu kwagala kwe n’obugabi bwe. |
| For a Spirit of Compassion | Okuba n'omwoyo gw'okusaasira |
| O tender Father of the poor who, when all other resources were exhausted, didst offer thyself for their relief, obtain for me that true spirit of compassion for the suffering members of Jesus Christ, which shone forth so conspicuously in thy holy life. | Omutonzi wa taata w'abavu, eyawaddeyo obulamu bwe okubayamba nga ebintu byonna bikendedde, nkusaba onnyambe nfune omwoyo ogw'obusaasizi eri abo abali mu bulumi mu Yesu Kristo, ogwalaga mu bulamu bwo obutukuvu. |
| May I, by charity to those whom Jesus so dearly loves, lay up for myself treasures in heaven, where thou now enjoyest that which the eye hath not seen, nor the ear heard, nor the heart of man conceived, but which God has prepared for those who love Him, and who prove themselves His Disciples by the observance of His Divine precept: love one another. | Nnyinza, nga nkozesa obulungi bwange eri abo Yesu b’ayagala ennyo, okuteekateeka eby’obugagga mu ggulu, gy’oli kati weewummulira ekyo amaaso gatakirabye, wadde okutu kwawulidde, wadde omutima gw’omuntu gwakitegedde, naye Katonda ky’ataddewo eri abamwagala era abakakasa nga bayizi be nga bakwata amateeka ge ag’obutukuvu: mwagalane. |
| I praise and thank God for the high degree of sanctity to which He had raised thee, and the special privileges by which He has distinguished thee. | Ntendereza era neebaza Katonda olw'obutukuvu obungi bwe yakuwa, n'enkizo ez'enjawulo z'akugabira. |
| I ask thee, by that gratitude with which thou shalt for all eternity be penetrated for thy Divine Benefactor, implore for me the grace to root out of my heart whatever is not agreeable in His sight, especially that evil habit by which I most frequently offend Him. | Nkwebuuza, olw'okusiima kwe onookozesa okumala emirembe gyonna okukusanyusa Omutonzi wo ow'ekitalo, nsaba ompe ekisa okuggya mu mutima gwange buli kimu kyonna ekitali kirungi mu maaso ge, naddala empisa embi gye nkola ennyo nga mmunyiiza. |
| Obtain likewise the favours I request in this Novena, through thy powerful intercession. | Funa mu ngeri y'emu ebirungi bye nsaba mu Novena eno, okuyitira mu kusabira kwo okw'amaanyi. |
| To Our Lady, Queen of the Rosary | Eri Mukama waffe, Kwiini w'Essaala ya Rozali |
| O glorious Mother of God, Queen of the most sacred Rosary, thou who didst love Dominic with the affection of a mother, and were most tenderly loved and honoured by him, look upon me, for his sake, with an eye of pity, deign to join with him in presenting these petitions to thy most Blessed Jesus. | O Mukama w'ekitiibwa, Maama wa Katonda, Kabaka w'Omusaalaba ogutukuvu ennyo, ggwe eyayagala Dominic ng'omukwano gw'omuzadde, era n'okwagalwa n'okuweebwa ekitiibwa mu maaso ge, ndabirira, ku lw'obulungi bwe, n'amaaso ag'ekisa, okwegatta naye mu kuleeta okusaba kuno eri Yesu wo omutukuvu. |
| I sincerely desire from this moment to love Him with all my heart, and serve Him with all my strength, and now place myself under thy powerful protection, as a sure means of obtaining all the graces necessary to serve Him faithfully here, that I may eternally rejoice with Him hereafter. Amen. | Nnyinza okwagala okuva kati okumwagala n'omutima gwange gwonna, era okumuweereza n'amaanyi gange gonna, era kati nteeka obulamu bwange mu bukuumi bwo obw'amaanyi, nga kino kyekyokukozesa okufuna ebirungi byonna ebyetaagisa okumuweereza mu bwesigwa wano, nsobole okusanyuka naye emirembe gyonna. Amiina. |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Sorrows | Okusaba eri Omukyala waffe ow'Enaku |
| O sorrowful Mother, I turn to you in total trust. You suffered the sharpest pains in life, watching your Son die upon the Cross, and yet you remained by Him to the end. | O Maama ow'ennaku, nkwesigamako mu bwesige obujjuvu. Wabonaabona ennyo mu bulamu, ng'olaba Omwana wo afa ku Musalaba, era wabangawo okutuuka ku nkomerero. |
| Look with favor upon me, a poor sinner, and obtain for me from your Son all the graces I need to endure the sufferings God allows me to face. | Nnyonera ekisa, omwonoonyi omwavu, era onfunire okuva mu Mwana wo ebirungi byonna bye nneetaaga okugumira okubonaabona Katonda kw'ankkiriza okwolekagana nakwo. |
| At the Cross Her Station Keeping | Ku Kkubo lye ery'Okusala |
| On the Cross her Son was dying. Mary stood beneath Him crying, Sharing in His saving cross. As He hangs, her soul is grieving, and a sword her heart is cleaving and she weeps the bitter loss. | Ku musalaba, Omwana we yali afiira. Malyamu yayimirira wansi We ng'akaaba, Ng'ayita mu musalaba ogw'okulokola kwe. Bwe yali agudde, emmeeme ye yali ewulira ennaku, Era ekitala kyakuba omutima gwe nga kimusala, N'akaaba olw'okufiirwa okunakuwaza. |
| The First Sorrow | Okunakuwala Okusooka |
| O Mother of Sorrows, through thy First Sorrow, the Prophecy of Holy Simeon, intercede for me with the Sacred Heart of Jesus, and grant me the favor I implore (mention request here…) | O Maama w'Okunakuwala, okuyita mu Kunakuwala kwo Okusooka, Obunnabbi bwa Simeoni Omutukuvu, onkwatireko n'Omutima omutukuvu gwa Yesu, era ompa ekisa kye nsaba (teekamu okusaba kwo wano…) |
| The Memorare | Memorare |
| Remember, O most gracious Virgin Mary, that never was it known that anyone who fled to your protection, implored your help or sought your intercession, was left unaided. Inspired by this confidence, I fly unto you, O Virgin of Virgins, my Mother. To you do I come, before you I stand, sinful and sorrowful. | Jjukira, O Mawulire Omugole Maryamu, nti tewali muntu yenna eyadduka okufuna obukuumi bwo, n’asaba obuyambi bwo oba n’anoonya okusabirwa kwo, eyalekebwa nga talina buyambi. Nga nsikiriziddwa ku bwesige buno, nkuleetera eri, O Mugole w’Abagole, Maama wange. Nkujja gyoli, mu maaso go nnyimiridde, nga ndi munyoomi era omunakuwavu. |
| O Mother of the Word Incarnate, despise not my petitions, but in your clemency, hear and answer me. Amen. | O Maama w'Ekigambo Ekifuuse Omuntu, tokolokota kusaba kwange, naye mu kisa kyo, mpuliriza era onziramu. Amiina. |
| Prayers to Our Lady of Guadalupe | Okusaba eri Mukama waffe ow'e Guadalupe |
| Patroness of the Americas | Omukulembeze w'Amawanga ga America |
| First Prayer | Essaala esooka |
| Our Lady Of Guadalupe, mystical rose, make intercession for the Holy Church; protect the Holy Father; help all those who invoke you in their needs. | Mukama waffe ow'e Guadalupe, omulanga ogw'ekyewuunyo, okwegayirira ku lwa Eklezia Entukuvu; okukuuma Kitaawe Omutukuvu; okuyamba bonna abakusaba mu byetaago byabwe. |
| Since you are the ever Virgin Mary and Mother of the true God, obtain for us from your most holy Son Jesus the grace of keeping our faith, sweet hope in the midst of the difficulties of life, burning charity, and the precious gift of final perseverance.
