| O Virgin of Guadalupe, you want to remain with us through your admirable Image, you who are our Mother, our health, and our life. | O Virgin wa Guadalupe, nooyenda kuguma naitwe kurabira omu kishushani kyawe ekirikusiimwa, iwe ori Maama waitu, amagara gaitu, hamwe n'amagara gaitu. |
| Placing ourselves beneath your maternal gaze, and having recourse to you in all our necessities, we need do nothing more. | Twaba twemereire ahansi y'okureeba kwawe nk'omukazi, kandi twaba twine obuhwezi bwawe omu byetengo byaitu byona, titwine ekindi ky'okukora. |
| O Holy Mother of God, despise not our petitions, but in your mercy hear and answer us. Amen. | O Nyina Ruhanga Orikwera, otarekye kushaba kw'aitu, kureka omu mbabazi zaawe ohurire kandi otugarukemu. Amen |
| Dearest Lady of Guadalupe, fruitful Mother of holiness, teach me your ways of gentleness and strength. Hear my humble prayer offered with heartfelt confidence to beg this favor. | Omukazi w'omuhendo wa Guadalupe, Omukazi w'amaani omu by'oburungi, nyegyesa emiringo yaawe y'obucureezi n'amaani. Hurira okushaba kwangye okw'obucureezi okurikuheebwa n'obwesigye bw'omutima kushaba obuhwezi obu. |
| Prayer to the Holy Name | Okushaba omu iziina eryera |
| O merciful Jesus! Who didst in Thine early infancy commence Thine office of Savior, by shedding Thy precious Blood and assuming for us that Name which is above all names; we thank Thee for such early proofs of Thine infinite love. | Yesu ow'embabazi! Nooha owaatandikire omurimo gwawe nk'omujuni obu waaba ori muto, orikushuka eshagama yaawe ey'omuhendo kandi okatwara eiziina eryo eriri ahaiguru y'amaziina goona; nitukusiima ahabw'obujurizi bw'okubanza obw'okukunda kwawe okutahwaho. |
| We venerate Thy sacred Name, in union with the profound respect of the Angel who first announced it to the earth, and unite our affections to the sentiments of tender devotion which the adorable Name of Jesus has in all ages enkindled in the hearts of Thy servants. | Nitwaramya eiziina ryawe eririkwera, omu bumwe n'ekitiinisa ky'amaani ekya Malaika owabandize kurirangirira omu nsi, kandi tukagaita okukunda kwaitu aha nyehurira z'okwehayo kw'omutima ogurikwetengwa ogu eiziina rya Yesu ery'omuhendo gw'ahaiguru ririho omu myaka yoona rikareetaho omu mitima y'abaheereza baawe. |
| Animated with a firm faith in Thine unerring word, and penetrated with confidence in Thy mercy, we now most humbly remind Thee of the promise Thou hast made, that when two or three should gather in Thy Name, Thou Thyself wouldst be in the midst of them. | Ahabw'okwikiriza omu kigambo kyawe ekitarikuhinduka, hamwe n'obwesigye omu mbabazi zaawe, hati nitukwijutsya omu bucureezi aha kiraganiso eki waakozire, ngu abantu babiri nainga bashatu ku barikuteeranira omu iziina ryawe, iwe wenka noija kuba ori ahagati yaabo. |
| Come, then, into the midst of us, most amiable Jesus! | Ija, hati, omu kati kaitu, Yesu orikukundwa munonga! |
| For it is in Thy Sacred Name we are here assembled. | Ahabwokuba omu iziina ryawe ery'ekitiinisa, nitwe twabugana aha. |
| Come into our hearts, that Thy Holy Spirit may pray in and by us; and mercifully grant us, through that adorable Name, which is the joy of heaven, the terror of hell, the consolation of the afflicted, and the solid ground of our unlimited confidence, all the petitions we make in this Novena. | Ija omu mitima yaitu, ngu Omwoyo waawe Orikwera asabe omuriitwe kandi ahabwaitu; kandi otuhe embabazi, kurabira omu iziina eryo eririkusiimwa, eri n'okushemererwa kw'omu iguru, okutiinisa kw'omu geyena, okuhuumuriza abarikubonabona, hamwe n'omusingye gw'obwesigye bwaitu obutarikuhwaho, okushaba kwona oku turikukora omu Novena egi. |
| O Blessed Mother of our Redeemer; who didst participate so deeply in the sufferings of thy dear Son, when He shed His sacred Blood, and assumed for us the Name of Jesus; obtain for us through that adorable Name, the favors we petition in this Novena. | O Maama Orikwera ow'omucunguzi waitu; owaayetabire munonga omu kubonabona kw'Omwana waawe ou akunda, obu yaashuka eshagama ye erikwera, kandi yaatutwarira eiziina rya Yesu; otutungire kurabira omu eiziina eryo eririkukundwa, obuhwezi obu turikushaba omuri Novena egi. |
| Beg, also, that the most ardent love may imprint on our hearts that sacred Name, that it may be always in our minds, and frequently on our lips; that it may be our defence in temptations, and our refuge in danger, during our lives, and our consolation and support in the hour of death. | Kandi, shaba ngu rukundo erikukirayo obw'amaani eshemereire kuta omu mitima yaitu eiziina eryo eririkwera, kugira ngu rishemereire kuba riri omu biteekateeko byaitu obwire bwona, kandi rikakira kugambwa aha minwa yaitu; kugira ngu rishemereire kuba niryo burinzi bwaitu omu kwohibwa, hamwe n'obuhungiro bwaitu omu kabi, omu magara gaitu, hamwe n'okuhuumuriza kwaitu hamwe n'obuhwezi omu shaaha y'okufa. |
| Litany of the Holy Name | Ekitabo ky'eiziina rya Ruhanga |
| V. Lord, have mercy on us. R. Christ, have mercy on us. | V. Mukama, tugirire embabazi. R. Kristo, tugirire embabazi. |
| V. Lord, have mercy on us. Jesus, hear us. R. Jesus, graciously hear us. | V. Mukama, twagirire embabazi. Yesu, tuhurira. R. Yesu, tuhurira omu mbabazi. |
| V. God the Father of Heaven, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Ruhanga, Taata w'omu iguru, R. Tutugirire embabazi. |
| V. God the Son, Redeemer of the world, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Ruhanga Omwana, omucunguzi w'ensi, R. Otugirire embabazi. |
| V. God the Holy Spirit, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Ruhanga omwoyo gurikwera, R. Tugirire embabazi. |
| V. Holy Trinity, one God, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Obutatu oburikwera, Ruhanga omwe, R. Twagirire embabazi. |
| V. Jesus, Son of the living God, R. Have mercy on us. | V. Yesu, Omwana wa Ruhanga ohuriire, R. Twagirire embabazi. |
| After each line, answer: have mercy on us. | Buri runyiriri, garukamu: otugirire embabazi. |
| Jesus, splendor of the Father. Jesus, brightness of eternal light. Jesus, King of glory. Jesus, sun of justice. Jesus, Son of the Virgin Mary. Jesus, most amiable. Jesus, most admirable. Jesus, the mighty God. Jesus, Father of the world to come. Jesus, angel of great counsel. Jesus, most powerful. Jesus, most patient. Jesus, most obedient. Jesus, meek and humble of heart. Jesus, lover of chastity. Jesus, lover of us. Jesus, God of peace. Jesus, author of life. Jesus, example of virtues. Jesus, zealous lover of souls. Jesus, our God. Jesus, our refuge. Jesus, father of the poor. Jesus, treasure of the faithful. Jesus, good Shepherd. Jesus, true light. Jesus, eternal wisdom. Jesus, infinite goodness. Jesus, our way and our life. Jesus, joy of Angels. Jesus, King of the Patriarchs. Jesus, Master of the Apostles. Jesus, teacher of the Evangelists. Jesus, strength of Martyrs. Jesus, light of Confessors. Jesus, purity of Virgins. Jesus, crown of Saints. | Yesu, oburungi bwa Ishe. Yesu, omushana gw'omushana ogutahwaho. Yesu, Omugabe w'ekitiinisa. Yesu, eizooba ry'oburingaaniza. Yesu, Omwana wa Mariamu Omukazi. Yesu, orikukirayo kuba murungi. Yesu, orikukirayo kuba ow'okureeberaho. Yesu, Ruhanga ow'amaani. Yesu, Ishe w'ensi erikwija. Yesu, maraika w'obuhabuzi bw'amaani. Yesu, orikukirayo amaani. Yesu, orikukirayo okugumisiriza. Yesu, orikukirayo okworobera. Yesu, omucureezi kandi omucureezi w'omutima. Yesu, omukundwa w'obuhangwa. Yesu, omukundwa waitu. Yesu, Ruhanga w'obusingye. Yesu, omuhandiiki w'amagara. Yesu, eky'okureeberaho ky'emitwarize mirungi. Yesu, omukundwa w'abakundwa b'emyoyo. Yesu, Ruhanga waitu. Yesu, obuhungiro bwaitu. Yesu, ishe w'abooro. Yesu, eby'obugaiga by'abeesigwa. Yesu, Omuriisa murungi. Yesu, omushana gw'amazima. Yesu, amagezi agatahwaho. Yesu, oburungi obutarikuhwaho. Yesu, omuhanda gwaitu hamwe n'amagara gaitu. Yesu, okushemererwa kw'Abamaraika. Yesu, Omugabe w'Abatandikirizi. Yesu, Omwegyesa w'Abajanjabi. Yesu, amaani g'Abajurizi. Yesu, omushana gw'Abajurizi. Yesu, oburungi bw'Abakazi abarikwetegyeka. Yesu, ekirunga ky'Abatukuvu. |
| V. Be merciful, R. spare us, O Jesus. | V. Gira embabazi, R. Otuhumure, Yesu. |
| V. Be merciful, R. graciously hear us, O Jesus. | V. Gira embabazi, R. otuhurire omu mbabazi, Yesu. |
| V. From all evil, R. deliver us, O Jesus. | V. Okuruga omu bibi byona, R. otujune, O Yesu. |
| After each line, answer: deliver us, O Jesus. | Bwanyima ya buri runyiriri, garukamu: otuheereze, O Yesu. |
| From all sin. From Your wrath. From the snares of the devil. From the spirit of fornication. From everlasting death. From the neglect of Your inspirations. By the mystery of Your holy Incarnation. By Your Nativity. By Your Infancy. By Your most divine Life. By Your labors. By Your agony and passion. By Your cross and dereliction. By Your sufferings. By Your death and burial. By Your Resurrection. By Your Ascension. By Your institution of the most Holy Eucharist. By Your joys. By Your glory. | Kuruga omu kibi kyona. Kuruga omu kihururu kyawe. Kuruga omu butego bw'omuregi. Kuruga omu mwoyo gw'obushambani. Kuruga omu kufa okutahwaho. Kuruga omu kwehuzya ebiteekateeko byawe. Ahabw'ekihama ky'okuhinduka kwawe okurikwera. Ahabw'okuzaarwa kwawe. Ahabw'obuto bwawe. Ahabw'amagara gaawe agari ahaiguru munonga. Ahabw'emirimo yaawe. Ahabw'obusaasi bwawe hamwe n'okugira ekiniga. Ahabw'omusaraba gwawe hamwe n'okurekyera aho. Ahabw'okubonabona kwawe. Ahabw'okufa kwawe hamwe n'okuziikwa kwawe. Ahabw'okuzooka kwawe. Ahabw'okugyenda kwawe omu iguru. Ahabw'okutandikaho kwawe Eukarisitiya erikwera munonga. Ahabw'ebishemererwa byawe. Ahabw'ekitiinisa kyawe. |
| V. Lamb of God, who takest away the sins of the world, R. spare us, O Jesus. | V. Lamb wa Ruhanga, orikwiha ebibi by'ensi, R. otuhumure, Yesu. |
| V. Lamb of God, who takest away the sins of the world, R. graciously hear us, O Jesus. | V. Lamb wa Ruhanga, orikwihaho ebibi by'ensi, R. otuhurire omu muringo gw'embabazi, Yesu. |
| V. Lamb of God, who takest away the sins of the world, R. have mercy on us, O Jesus. | V. Lamb wa Ruhanga, orikwihaho ebibi by'ensi, R. otugirire embabazi, Yesu. |
| V. Jesus, hear us. R. Jesus, graciously hear us. | V. Yesu, tuhurire. R. Yesu, mu mbabazi tuhurire. |
| O Lord Jesus Christ, Thou hast said, Ask and you shall receive, seek, and you shall find, knock, and it shall be opened to you. | Mukama Yesu Kristo, ogambire oti, shaba kandi noija kutunga, ronda kandi noija kushanga, konkona kandi noija kwigurirwa. |
| Grant, we beg Thee, to us who ask it, the gift of Thy most divine love, that we may ever love Thee with our whole heart, in word and deed, and never cease praising Thee. | Grant, nitushaba Ruhanga, ahariitwe abarikukishaba, ekiconco ky'okukunda kwe okw'oburungi bwingi, ngu tubaase kukundakwe n'omutima gwaitu gwona, omu kigambo hamwe n'omu bikorwa, kandi tutarikuhwa kuhimbisa Ruhanga. |
| Give us, O Lord, as much a lasting fear as a lasting love of Thy Holy Name, for Thee, who live and are King for ever and ever, never fail to govern those whom Thou have solidly established in Thy love. | Tuheereze, Mukama waitu, okutiina okurikugumaho nk'oku orikukunda eiziina ryawe eririkwera, ahabwokuba iwe, orikubaho kandi ori Omugabe ebiro byona n'ebyo, torikubaasa kuremwa kutegyeka abo abu waataireho omu kukunda kwawe. |
| Mother of Holy Hope | Maama w'okwikiriza okurikwera |
| I am the mother of fair love, and of fear, and of knowledge, and of holy hope. In me is all grace of the way and of the truth; in me is all hope of life and of virtue. Come to me all that desire me and be filled with my fruits (Sirach 24:24-26). | Ndi nyina w'okukunda okurungi, hamwe n'okutiina, hamwe n'obwengye, hamwe n'amatsiko marungi. Omu nyowe harimu embabazi zoona ez'omuhanda hamwe n'amazima; omu nyowe harimu amatsiko goona ag'amagara hamwe n'emitwarize mirungi. Ija aha nyowe abo boona abarikunyenda kandi obaase kujwiibwa ebijuma byangye (Sirach 24:24-26). |
| O Blessed Virgin Mary, Mother of Grace, Hope of the world. Hear us, your children, who cry to you. | O Blessed Virgin Mary, Maama w'embabazi, amatsiko g'ensi. Twihurire, abaana baawe, abarikukweta. |
| O God, who by the marvelous protection of the Blessed Virgin Mary has strengthened us firmly in hope, grant we beseech You, that by persevering in prayer at her admonition, we may obtain the favors we devoutly implore. Through Christ Our Lord. | Ai Ruhanga, orikurinda omu muringo gw'okutangaaza ogwa Mariya Omukama w'ekitiinisa otugumize munonga omu kubaasa, twakushaba ngu, turikuguma nitushaba aha buhabuzi bwe, tubaase kutunga obuhwezi obu turikweshengyereza. Kurabira omuri Kristo Mukama waitu. |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Hope | Okushaba kwa Nyina waitu ow'Amatsiko |
| O Mary, my Mother, I kneel before you with heavy heart. The burden of my sins oppresses me. The knowledge of my weakness discourages me. I am beset by fears and temptations of every sort. Yet I am so attached to the things of this world that instead of longing for Heaven I am filled with dread at the thought of death. | O Mary, Maama wangye, Ninkutama omu maisho gaawe n'omutima ogurikuremera. Omugugu gw'ebibi byangye nigunyigiriza. Okumanya obweremwa bwangye nikunshobera. Nimpikirizibwa okutiina hamwe n'okwohibwa kw'emiringo yoona. Kwonka ninyine enkoragana y'amaani n'ebintu by'ensi egi ekirikumanyisa ngu omu mwanya gw'okwenda kugyenda omu iguru nimpikirizibwa okutiina ahabw'ekiteekateeko ky'okufa. |
| O Mother of Mercy, have pity on me in my distress. You are all-powerful with your Divine Son. He can refuse no request of your Immaculate Heart. Show yourself a true Mother to me by being my advocate before His throne. O Refuge of Sinners and Hope of the Hopeless, to whom shall I turn if not you? | O Maama w'embabazi, nyikiriza obusaasi bwangye omu kubonabona kwangye. Oine amaani goona n'Omwana waawe ow'obuhangwa. Tarabaasa kugaana ekishaba kyona eky'omutima gwawe ogutarimu kibi. Nyoreka ngu ori Maama w'amazima ahariinye orikubaasa kuba omurwanirizi wangye omu maisho g'entebe ye. O Obuhungiro bw'Abasiisi kandi Amatsiko g'Abatarikugira matsiko, ninyija kuhindukira oha ku oraabe otari iwe? |
| Obtain for me, then, O Mother of Hope, the grace of true sorrow for my sins, the gift of perfect resignation to God’s Holy Will, and the courage to take up my cross and follow Jesus. Beg of His Sacred Heart the special favor that I ask in this novena. | Nkushaba, nyina w'okuhuumuriza, obugabe bw'okusaasira kw'amazima ahabw'ebibi byangye, ekiconco ky'okurekurira kimwe omu kigyendererwa kya Ruhanga eky'obutukuvu, hamwe n'obumanzi bw'okutwara omusaraba gwangye n'okukuratira Yesu. Shaba omutima gwe ogurikwera obuhwezi obw'omutaano obu ndikushaba omu novena egi. |
| But above all I pray, O dearest Mother, that through your most powerful intercession my heart may be filled with Holy Hope, so that in life’s darkest hour I may never fail to trust in God my Saviour, but by walking in the way of His commandments I may merit to be united with Him, and with you in the eternal joys of Heaven. | Kwonka okukira byona nshaba, O maama wange omukundwa,  ngu kurabira omu kushaba kwawe okw'amaani omutima gwangye gushemereire kujurirwa amatsiko marungi, kugira ngu omu bwire bw'amagara oburikukirayo oburemeezi ntakagume kugumizamu ninteekateeka ahari Ruhanga Omujuni wangye, kwonka ndikugyenda omu muhanda gw'ebiragiro bye nimbasa kuhikiiriza kuba hamwe nawe, hamwe naiwe omu mashemererwa agatahwaho aga Iguru. |
| Mary, our Hope, have pity on us. | Mary, amatsiko gaitu, atworekye embabazi. |
| Hope of the Hopeless, pray for us. | Amatsiko g'abataine matsiko, mutushabire. |
| Pray this prayer once a day for 30 days | Shaba eshaara egi omurundi gumwe buri izooba kumara ebiro 30 |
| Pray the same prayer each day, for 30 days in a row. | Shaba eshaara emwe buri izooba, okumara ebiro 30 byona. |
| Prayer to St. Joseph | Okushaba St. Joseph |
| Ever blessed and glorious Joseph, kind and loving father, and helpful friend of all in sorrow! You are the good father and protector of orphans, the defender of the defenseless, the patron of those in need and sorrow. Look kindly on my request. My sins have drawn down on me the just displeasure of my God, and so I am surrounded with unhappiness. To you, loving guardian of the Family of Nazareth, do I go for help and protection. | Joseph owabaire ari omugisha kandi ow'ekitiinisa, taata w'embabazi kandi orikukunda, hamwe n'omunywani orikuhwera abo boona abari omu busaasi! Ori taata murungi kandi omurwanirizi w'efuuzi, omurwanirizi w'abarikwetenga obuhwezi, omurwanirizi w'abo abarikwetenga obuhwezi hamwe n'obusaasi. Reeba embabazi omu kushaba kwangye. Ebibi byangye bitungire okutashemererwa kwa Ruhanga wangye, nahabwekyo nshwekirwe obutashemererwa. Aha iwe, omurwanirizi w'eka ya Nazareth, niinyija kukushaba obuhwezi hamwe n'oburinzi. |
| Listen, then, I beg you, with fatherly concern, to my earnest prayers, and obtain for me the favors I ask. | Muhurikize, nimbashaba, ahabw'okufayo kwa taata, okushaba kwangye okw'amazima, kandi mungarukiremu obuheereza obu ndikushaba. |
| I Ask It | Ninkabuuza |
| I ask it by the infinite mercy of the eternal Son of God, which moved Him to take our nature and to be born into this world of sorrow. | Ninkishaba ahabw'embabazi zitarikuhwa ez'omwana wa Ruhanga ow'obutwire, owayehaireyo kutwara obuhangwa bwaitu n'okuzaarirwa omunsi egi y'okushoberwa. |
| I ask it by the weariness and suffering you endured when you found no shelter at the inn of Bethlehem for the holy Virgin, nor a house where the Son of God could be born. Then, being everywhere refused, you had to allow the Queen of Heaven to give birth to the world’s Redeemer in a cave. | Ninkibuuza ahabw'oburuhe n'okubonabona oku waagumisiriza obu waashanga otaine butaaho omu nju ya Bethlehem ahabwa Omukazi orikwera, kandi otaine nju ei Omwana wa Ruhanga yaabaire naabaasa kuzaarirwamu. Obwo, obwo orikwanga buri hamwe, okaba oine kwikiriza Omugabekazi w'omu Iguru kuzaara Omucunguzi w'ensi omu mpuku. |
| I ask it by the loveliness and power of that sacred Name, Jesus, which you conferred on the adorable infant. | Ninkibuuza omu rukundo n'amaani g'eiziina eryo eririkwera, Yesu, eri waheire omwana w'omukazi. |
| I ask it by that painful torture you felt at the prophecy of holy Simeon, which declared the Child Jesus and His holy Mother future victims of our sins and of their great love for us. | Ninkibuuza kurabira omu kubonabonesa okwabaireho ahabw'okugamba kw'obunabi bwa Simeoni orikwera, okwarangirire ngu Omwana Yesu hamwe na nyina we orikwera bakaba nibaija kuba abarikubonabonesibwa ebibi byaitu hamwe n'okukunda kwabo okw'amaani ahariitwe. |
| I ask it through your sorrow and pain of soul when the angel declared to you that the life of the Child Jesus was sought by His enemies. From their evil plan you had to flee with Him and His Blessed Mother to Egypt. I ask it by all the suffering, weariness, and labors of that long and dangerous journey. | Ninkibuuza kurabira omu kushoberwa kwawe hamwe n'obusaasi bw'omutima obu maraika yaarangirira ahariiwe ngu amagara g'omwana Yesu gakaba nigasherurwa abazigu be. Kuruga omu nteekateeka yaabo embi okaba oine kuhunga nawe hamwe na nyina we owabaire ari omugisha kuza Misiri. Ninkibuuza kurabira omu kubonabona kwona, oburuhe, hamwe n'emirimo y'orugyendo orwo oruraihire kandi orw'akabi. |
| I ask it by all your care to protect the Sacred Child and His Immaculate Mother during your second journey, when you were ordered to return to your own country. I ask it by your peaceful life in Nazareth where you met with so many joys and sorrows. | Ninkishaba omu kureeberera kwawe kwona okurinda Omwana w'ekitiinisa hamwe na Nyina we ow'obutaine kamogo omu rugyendo rwawe orwa kabiri, obu waaragiirwe kugaruka omu ihanga ryawe. Ninkishaba omu magara gaawe ag'obusingye omuri Nazareth ahu waabugaine n'okushemererwa kwingi hamwe n'obusaasi bwingi. |
| I ask it by your great distress when the adorable Child was lost to you and His Mother for three days. I ask it by your joy at finding Him in the Temple, and by the comfort you found at Nazareth, while living in the company of the Child Jesus. I ask it by the wonderful submission He showed in His obedience to you. | Ninkibuuza ahabw'obusaasi bwawe oburikukirayo obu Omwana ow'okusiimwa yaabura ahariiwe hamwe na Nyina kumara ebiro bishatu. Ninkibuuza ahabw'okushemererwa kwawe okw'okumushanga omu hekalu, hamwe n'okuhuumurizibwa oku waatungire omuri Nazareeti, obu waabaire nootuurana n'omwana Yesu. Ninkibuuza ahabw'okworobera okurikutangaaza oku yaayorekire omu kworoba kwe ahariiwe. |
| I ask it by the perfect love and conformity you showed in accepting the Divine order to depart from this life, and from the company of Jesus and Mary. I ask it by the joy which filled your soul, when the Redeemer of the world, triumphant over death and hell, entered into the possession of His kingdom and led you into it with special honors. | Ninkibuuza omu rukundo rw'amaani hamwe n'okwikirizana oku waayorekire omu kwikiriza entebeekanisa y'Obwa Ruhanga kushohora omu magara aga, hamwe n'omu kibiina kya Yesu na Mariya. Ninkibuuza omu kushemererwa okwabaire kwiijwire omu mutima gwawe, obu Omucunguzi w'ensi, arikusingura okufa hamwe n'omuriro ogutaraara, yaataaha omu bugabe bwe kandi yaakwebembera omuri bwo n'ekitiinisa ky'omutaano. |
| I ask it through Mary’s glorious Assumption, and through that endless happiness you have with her in the presence of God. | Ninkishaba kurabira omuri Mary ow'ekitiinisa ky'okutwara Ruhanga, kandi kurabira omu okushemererwa okutarikuhwa oku oine nawe omu maisho ga Ruhanga. |
| O good father! I beg you, by all your sufferings, sorrows, and joys, to hear me and obtain for me what I ask. | Otaata murungi! Ninkushaba, ahabw'okubonabona kwawe kwona, obusaasi bwawe, hamwe n'okushemererwa kwawe, ngu ompurikize kandi onshabire eki ndikushaba. |
| Obtain for all those who have asked my prayers everything that is useful to them in the plan of God. Finally, my dear patron and father, be with me and all who are dear to me in our last moments, that we may eternally sing the praises of Jesus, Mary and Joseph. | Muhindure abo boona abaashabire okushaba kwangye buri kimwe ekirabasa kubayamba omu nteekateeka ya Ruhanga. Aha muheru, omukundwa wangye kandi taata, guma naiwe hamwe naabo boona abarikunkunda omu bwire bw'ahamuheru, ngu tubaase kweshongora ebyeshongoro bya Yesu, Mariya na Yozefu ebiro byona. |
| O God, who said let the little children come to Me and who love children, please answer the prayers I ask in this novena through the saints’ intercession, especially | Ai Ruhanga, owaagambire ngu abaana bato baije ahariinye kandi orikukunda abaana, nkweshengyereza garukamu okushaba kwangye omu novena egi kurabira omu kuhurikiriza kwa baahure, namunonga |
| Prayer to Our Blessed Mother | Okushaba kwa maama waitu ow'omugisha |
| Most Holy Mother, hear the cries of my heart right now. Blessed Virgin, I want to be fruitful, but I have not yet been blessed with fertility. Grant me fertility over my life and end my suffering. | Omukazi Orikwera, hurira okurira kw'omutima gwangye hati. Omukazi w'ekitiinisa, ninyenda kuba ow'okubanza, kwonka tinkabaire nimbwa omugisha gw'okuzaara. Nkaha omugisha gw'okuzaara aha magara gangye kandi omaririze okubonabona kwangye. |
| Help make me a fruitful vine within my house. My children will be like olive shoots around my table. Mother of Mercy, I know I was not created so that I would be fruitless, but your Son created me to be effective in all that I do. I know that my season of fertility will come according to your Son’s timing. Amen. | Mpereza okundetera omuzabibu ogurikwera omu nju yangye. Abaana bangye nibaija kuba nk'ebishushani by'emizeyituuni aha meeza yangye. Maama w'embabazi, nimanya ngu tindahangirwe kugira ngu ndebe ekirikuruga omu kukora kwangye, kwonka Omwana waawe akandehanga kuba ow'omugasho omuri buri kimwe eki ndikukora. Nimanya ngu obwire bwangye bw'okuhingura nibwija kuhika kurugiirira aha bwire bw'Omwana waawe. Amiina. |
| Prayer to St. Gerard Majella | Okushaba kwa St. Gerard Majella |
| O good St Gerard, powerful intercessor before God and wonder worker of our day, confidently I call upon you and seek your aid. | Ow'omugisha St Gerard, omuhwezi w'amaani omu maisho ga Ruhanga kandi omukozi w'ebihangirwe by'obunaku bwaitu, ninkushaba ndi n'obwesigye kandi nshaba obuhwezi bwawe. |
| You always fulfilled God’s designs, help me now to do the holy will of God. Implore the Master of Life, from whom all parenthood proceeds, to render me fruitful in offspring, that I may raise up children to God in this life, and in the world to come, heirs to the Kingdom of His Glory. Amen. | Buriijo okahikiiriza enteekateeka ya Ruhanga, nyamba hati kukora ebyetengo bya Ruhanga ebirikwera. Shaba Mukama w'amagara, ahu obuzaire bwona burikuruga, kumpa abaana baingi, ngu nshoboorore abaana ba Ruhanga omu magara aga, hamwe n'omu nsi erikwija, abaragwa b'Obukama bw'ekitiinisa Kye. Amiina. |
| Prayer to Sts. Joachim and Anne | Okushaba kwa Sts. Joachim na Anne |
| Most Holy Saints Anne and Joachim, come close to us and guide our hand and our hearts in the way of virtue. | Abatukuvu abarikwera munonga Anne na Yowakimu, mwije haihi naitwe kandi mutungye emikono yaitu hamwe n'emitima yaitu omumuhanda gw'oburungi. |
| Help us to conceive a child so that we may parent them with a gentle heart as you did. | Tuhwere kuteekateeka omwana kugira ngu tumureeberere n'omutima murungi nk'oku waakozire. |
| Obtain from God the grace to overcome all infertility so that we may raise our children in faith and bring our families into the Light of Jesus. | Tunga omugisha kuruga ahari Ruhanga kusingura obutazaara, tureebe ngu abaana baitu tubareeberera omu kwikiriza kandi tworekye amaka gaitu gatungye omushana gwa Yesu. |
| Sts Anne and Joachim, we place our petitions in your hands and ask that you lay them before the child Jesus. Amen. | Sts Anne na Joachim, nituta okushaba kwaitu omu mikono yaanyu kandi nitushaba ngu mukutware omu maisho ga Yesu omwana. Amen. |
| Prayer to St. Colette | Okushaba St. Colette |
| O glorious St. Colette, I beseech you through the burning love which inflamed your heart for Jesus in the Most Blessed Sacrament, through the marvelous graces with which He enriched you, and through the loving compassion you did bear to Him in His bitter sufferings, obtain from Him for me my request for fertility which I hope to receive through your powerful intercession. Amen. | Ow'ekitiinisa St. Colette, ninkushaba kurabira omu rukundo erikwotsya eyareteire omutima gwawe kugira okukunda Yesu omu Sacrament erikukirayo kuba ey'omugisha, kurabira omu migisha y'omutaano ei Yaakuhaire, hamwe n'embabazi z'okukunda ezi waamugiriire omu kubonabona kwe okwabaire kurimu obusaasi, ongambire aha bikwatiraine n'okushaba kwangye okw'okuzaara eki ndikwenda kutunga kurabira omu kushaba kwawe okw'amaani. Amen. |
| Prayer to St. Raphael to Heal Infertility | Okushaba St. Raphael okukiza obutazaara |
| Glorious Archangel St. Raphael, great prince of the heavenly court, you are illustrious for your gifts of wisdom and grace. | Omukwenda w'amaani St. Raphael, omunyiginya mukuru w'ekitongore ky'omu iguru, ori ow'omutaano ahabw'ebiconco byawe eby'obwengye hamwe n'embabazi. |
| You are a guide of those who journey by land or sea or air, consoler of the afflicted, and refuge of sinners. I beg you, assist me in all my needs and in all the sufferings of this life, as once you helped the young Tobias on his travels. | Ori omwebembezi w'abo abarikugyenda aha itaka nainga aha nyanja nainga omu mwanya, omuhuumuriza w'abarikubonabona, kandi obuhungiro bw'abasiisi. Ninkushaba, nyamba omu byetaago byangye byona hamwe n'omu kubonabona kw'amagara aga, nk'oku waahwereire Tobias omuto omu rugyendo rwe. |
| Because you are the Medicine of God, I humbly pray you to heal the many infirmities of my soul and the ills that afflict my body, especially of infertility. | Ahabw'okuba ori omubazi gwa Ruhanga, ndakushaba n'obucureezi ngu okize oburwaire bwingi bw'omutima gwangye hamwe n'endwara ezirikubonabonesa omubiri gwangye, okukira munonga obutazaara. |
| I also ask for the great grace of purity to prepare me to be the temple of the Holy Spirit. | Nimbaasa kushaba embabazi z'okuba oshemeire, kungarura omu mbeera y'okuba ekyombeko ky'omwoyo gurikwera. |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Fatima | Okushaba kw'omukazi waitu ow'e Fatima |
| Most Holy Virgin, who deigned to come to Fatima to reveal to the three little shepherds the treasures of graces hidden in the recitation of the Rosary, inspire our hearts with a sincere love of this devotion, so that by meditating on the mysteries of our redemption that are recalled in it, we may gather the fruits and obtain the conversion of sinners, the conversion of Russia and this favor which I so earnestly seek, | Ow'omutaano Omukazi Orikwera, owahaireyo kwija Fatima kushoboororera abashaija bashatu bato abariisa eby'obugaiga by'embabazi ebisherekirwe omu kugarukamu kwa Rosary, aheereze emitima yaitu okukunda okurungi okw'okwehayo oku, kugira ngu turikuteekateeka aha by'okucungurwa kwaitu ebirikwijukwa omuri kwo, tubaase kurundaana ebijuma kandi tufune okuhinduka kw'abasiisi, okuhinduka kwa Russia hamwe n'ekigyendererwa eki ndikuronda n'amaani maingi, |
| which I ask of thee in this novena, for the greater Glory of God, for thine own honor and for the good of all people. | eki ndikukushaba omu novena egi, ahabw'ekitiinisa kya Ruhanga ekirikukirayo obwingi, ahabw'ekitiinisa kyawe hamwe n'oburungi bw'abantu boona. |
| Prayer of Reparation | Okushaba kw'okugaruraho |
| My God, I believe, I adore, I hope and I love You! I ask pardon of You for those who do not believe, do not adore, do not hope and do not love You. Most Holy Trinity, Father, Son and Holy Spirit, I adore You profoundly, and I offer You the most precious Body, Blood, Soul and Divinity of Jesus Christ, present in all the tabernacles of the world, in reparation for the outrages, sacrileges and indifference with which He Himself is offended. And, through the infinite merits of His most Sacred Heart, and the Immaculate Heart of Mary, I beg of You the conversion of poor sinners. | Ruhanga wangye, ninyikiriza, ninkunda munonga, ninteekateeka kandi ninkukunda! Ninkushaba okusaasirwa ahabw'abo abatarikwikiriza, abatarikukunda munonga, abatarikuteekateeka kandi abatarikukunda. Ruhanga Orikwera Omurundi Gwakashatu, Taata, Omwana hamwe n'Omwoyo Orikwera, ninkukunda munonga, kandi ninkuha Omubiri ogurikukirayo oburungi, Esagama, Omwoyo hamwe n'Oburungi bwa Yesu Kristo, oburi omu myanya yoona y'ensi, omu kuhayo ahabw'okutwarwa kubi, okujumirirwa hamwe n'obutafayo obu Arikugambwaho. Kandi, kurabira omu migasho etarikuhwaho ey'omutima gwe ogurikwera munonga, hamwe n'omutima ogutarimu kibi ogwa Mariya, ninkushaba ngu ohindure abasiisi abooro. |
| Our Father, Hail Mary Glory Be (three times each) | Taata waitu, Hail Mary Ekitiinisa kibe (emirundi eshatu buri kamwe) |
| Prayer to St. Dominic | Okushaba kwa St. Dominic |
| O renowned champion of the faith of Christ, most holy saint Dominic, who didst renounce the honour and dignity of an earthly principality to embrace the poor, laborious, and mortified life which should distinguish a disciple of Him Who has said: If any man will come after Me, let him take up his cross and follow Me. | Omweshongozi w'amaani ow'okwikiriza kwa Kristo, omuramuzi orikwera munonga Dominic, owaayehaireyo okurekura ekitiinisa n'obugabe bw'obutegyeki bw'ensi kugira ngu akwatanise n'abooro, abarikukora munonga, hamwe n'amagara g'okubonabonesibwa agarikushemereire kutaanisa omwegi wa We Owaagambire ati: Omuntu weena ku araabe naayenda kwija enyima yangye, areke atware omusaraba gwe kandi amukurate. |
| O burning torch, who being thyself consumed with the fire of divine love, didst incessantly labour to enkindle that sacred flame in the hearts of others, look down upon me from that throne of glory where thou enjoyest the reward of all thy labours, and obtain that some sparks of that blessed fire may be lighted in my soul, to animate and encourage me under any crosses or trials with which it shall please the divine goodness to visit me. | Otoro y'okwosya, orikuba ori iwe wenka oyokirwe omuriro gw'okukunda kwa Ruhanga, okakora butoosha kushemeza omuriro ogwo ogurikwera omu mitima y'abandi, reeba ahansi aha ntebe y'ekitiinisa ei orikushemerererwamu omugisha gw'emirimo yaawe yoona, kandi otungye ngu obucweka bw'omuriro ogwo ogurikwera bugume bukashemereire omu mutima gwangye, kugira ngu buhimbise kandi buhagarike omu mihanda yoona nainga okugyezibwa oku kirikushemeza oburungi bwa Ruhanga kubungira. |
| Thou, O great Saint, regardest as nothing all the afflictions thou didst endure, and all the toils thou didst undergo for the promotion of God’s holy cause; obtain, I beseech Thee, that the same ardent love which strengthened thee, may make sweet to me labours, humiliations, disgraces, or whatever other mortifications I may have to suffer for the Name of Jesus. | Iwe, O Saint mukuru, nooreeba nk'ekitari kikuru okubonabona kwona oku waagumireho, hamwe n'okukora munonga oku waakozire ahabw'okutunguura omurimo gwa Ruhanga ogurikwera; nkweshengyereza, ngu rukundo ei yaakuhaire amaani, ekunde kushemeza emirimo yangye, okuswazibwa, okuswazibwa, nainga okubonabona okundi kwona oku ndikubaasa kubonabona ahabw'eiziina rya Yesu. |
| May I ever bear in mind that nothing can happen to me but by the particular dispensation of a God Who is infinitely wise, and therefore knows what is best for my welfare; infinitely powerful, and consequently able to effect it; and above all, infinitely merciful and loving, Who has laid down His life for my redemption, and continues daily to give new proofs of His love and bounty. | Nimbaasa kuguma ninteekateeka ngu tihariho ekirikubaasa kubaho ahariinye okwihaho kurabira omu muringo gw'omutaano ogwa Ruhanga orikwetegyereza munonga, kandi ahabw'ekyo naamanya ekirikukirayo oburungi ahabw'oburungi bwangye; ow'amaani munonga, kandi ahabw'ekyo naabaasa kukikora; kandi ekirikukirayo byona, ow'embabazi n'okukunda munonga, owaataireho amagara ge ahabw'okucungurwa kwangye, kandi akagumizamu buriizooba kuheereza obuhame busya bw'okukunda kwe hamwe n'embabazi ze. |
| For a Spirit of Compassion | Ahabw'omwoyo gw'embabazi |
| O tender Father of the poor who, when all other resources were exhausted, didst offer thyself for their relief, obtain for me that true spirit of compassion for the suffering members of Jesus Christ, which shone forth so conspicuously in thy holy life. | O taata w'abooro owaahaireyo, obu ebindi byona byahwaho, akahaayo ahabwabo kubahuumuriza, mpisye omwoyo gw'amazima ogw'embabazi aha bantu ba Yesu Kristo abarikubonabona, ogwabaire guriho omu magara gaawe amagororo. |
| May I, by charity to those whom Jesus so dearly loves, lay up for myself treasures in heaven, where thou now enjoyest that which the eye hath not seen, nor the ear heard, nor the heart of man conceived, but which God has prepared for those who love Him, and who prove themselves His Disciples by the observance of His Divine precept: love one another. | Nimbaasa, kurabira omu kugaba aha bantu abu Yesu akunda munonga, nteekye eby'obutungi omu iguru, ahu orikushemererwa hati ebi amaisho gataraareebire, nainga okutu kutahuriire, nainga omutima gw'omuntu gutarateekateeka, kwonka eki Ruhanga atebeekanisiize abarikumukunda, kandi abarikworeka ngu nibo Beegi be barikurinda ebiragiro bye by'Obuhangwa: kukundana. |
| I praise and thank God for the high degree of sanctity to which He had raised thee, and the special privileges by which He has distinguished thee. | Ninshaba kandi ninsiima Ruhanga ahabw'okukuhindura omuntu w'omugasho munonga, hamwe n'emigisha y'omutaano ei yaakuhaire. |
| I ask thee, by that gratitude with which thou shalt for all eternity be penetrated for thy Divine Benefactor, implore for me the grace to root out of my heart whatever is not agreeable in His sight, especially that evil habit by which I most frequently offend Him. | Ninkushaba, ahabw'okusiima oku orikwija kuguma oine obwire bwona ahabwa Omuhangi waawe ow'Oburungi, nshaba ngu ompabure embabazi z'okwiha omu mutima gwangye ekintu kyona ekitarikwikirizibwa omu maisho ge, okukira munonga omuze ogwo ogubi ogu ndikukira kumushiisha. |
| Obtain likewise the favours I request in this Novena, through thy powerful intercession. | Kandi oshabe okushaba kwangye omu Novena egi, kurabira omu kushaba kwaawe okw'amaani. |
| To Our Lady, Queen of the Rosary | Ahari Mukama waitu, Omugabekazi w'Ekirabo |
| O glorious Mother of God, Queen of the most sacred Rosary, thou who didst love Dominic with the affection of a mother, and were most tenderly loved and honoured by him, look upon me, for his sake, with an eye of pity, deign to join with him in presenting these petitions to thy most Blessed Jesus. | O nyina w'ekitiinisa, Omugabekazi w'omusaraba ogurikwera munonga, iwe owaakunzire Dominic n'okukunda kw'omukazi, kandi akakundwa munonga kandi yaheebwa ekitiinisa, ndeeba ahabwe, n'amaisho g'obusaasi, okikirize kwegaita nawe omu kushaba okushaba oku ahari Yesu waawe owahairwe omugisha. |
| I sincerely desire from this moment to love Him with all my heart, and serve Him with all my strength, and now place myself under thy powerful protection, as a sure means of obtaining all the graces necessary to serve Him faithfully here, that I may eternally rejoice with Him hereafter. Amen. | Ninyenda munonga kuruga omu bwire obu kukunda We n'omutima gwangye gwona, kandi ndikumuheereza n'amaani gangye goona, kandi hati nintaho oburinzi bwawe obw'amaani, nk'omuringo ogurikwesigwa gw'okutunga emigisha yoona erikwetengwa kumuheereza omu bwesigwa aha, ngu mpore okushemererwa nawe omu biro byona. Amen. |
| Prayer to Our Lady of Sorrows | Okushaba kwaitu Nyamukama ow'obusaasi |
| O sorrowful Mother, I turn to you in total trust. You suffered the sharpest pains in life, watching your Son die upon the Cross, and yet you remained by Him to the end. | Maama w'obusaasi, ninkuhindukira omu bwesigye bwona. Okabonabona obusaasi oburikukirayo omu magara gaawe, orikureeba Omwana waawe arikufa aha Musaraba, kandi okaguma ori nawe kuhisya aha muheru. |
| Look with favor upon me, a poor sinner, and obtain for me from your Son all the graces I need to endure the sufferings God allows me to face. | Mpa okushemererwa, omusiisi w'obusaasi, kandi ongabire kuruga aha Mwana waawe obugabe bwona obu ndikwetenga kugumisiriza okubonabona oku Ruhanga arikunyikiriza kubonabona. |
| At the Cross Her Station Keeping | Aha kisharara ky'omwanya gw'okurindiramu |
| On the Cross her Son was dying. Mary stood beneath Him crying, Sharing in His saving cross. As He hangs, her soul is grieving, and a sword her heart is cleaving and she weeps the bitter loss. | Aha musaraba, Omwana we akaba ariyo naafa. Mariamu akemera ahansi ye arikurira, Arikugabana omu musaraba gw'okukiza kwe. Obu yaabaire ayehingirire, omwoyo gwe gukaba nigushasha, kandi ekitala kikaba nikishara omutima gwe, kandi akarira okufeerwa okw'obusaasi. |
| The First Sorrow | Okushoberwa kw'okubanza |
| O Mother of Sorrows, through thy First Sorrow, the Prophecy of Holy Simeon, intercede for me with the Sacred Heart of Jesus, and grant me the favor I implore (mention request here…) | O Maama w'Okushoberwa, kurabira omu Kushoberwa kwawe okw'okubanza, Okuragura kwa Simeoni Orikwera, ongambire n'omutima gwa Yesu ogurikwera, kandi ompa embabazi ezi ndikushaba |
| The Memorare | Ekiijusyo |
| Remember, O most gracious Virgin Mary, that never was it known that anyone who fled to your protection, implored your help or sought your intercession, was left unaided. Inspired by this confidence, I fly unto you, O Virgin of Virgins, my Mother. To you do I come, before you I stand, sinful and sorrowful. | Ijuka, O Mariamu Omukazi w'ekitiinisa, ngu tikirikamanywa ngu omuntu weena owahungiire omu burinzi bwawe, akashaba obuhwezi bwawe nainga akasherura okushaba kwawe, akasigara ataine buhwezi. Ahabw'okugira obwesigye obu, ninkwirukirira, O Mariamu w'Abakazi b'Obusugi, Maama wangye. Ninkwija ahariiwe, omu maisho gaawe ndiyo nyemereire, ndi omunyabibi kandi ndi omu kuhwa amaani. |
| O Mother of the Word Incarnate, despise not my petitions, but in your clemency, hear and answer me. Amen. | O Maama w'ekigambo ekirugire omu kishushani, otashisha okushaba kwangye, kureka omu busingye bwawe, mpurira kandi ongambire. Amen. |
| Prayers to Our Lady of Guadalupe | Okushaba kwa Mukama waitu wa Guadalupe |
| Patroness of the Americas | Omukazi w'obutegyeki omu mahanga ga Amerika |
| First Prayer | Okushaba okw'okubanza |
| Our Lady Of Guadalupe, mystical rose, make intercession for the Holy Church; protect the Holy Father; help all those who invoke you in their needs. | Omukama waitu wa Guadalupe, orushozi rw'omutaano, shaba ahabwa Ekanisa Entoore; rinda omubiri gw'omureeberezi w'ekanisa; yamba abo boona abarikukushaba omu byetaago byabo. |
| Since you are the ever Virgin Mary and Mother of the true God, obtain for us from your most holy Son Jesus the grace of keeping our faith, sweet hope in the midst of the difficulties of life, burning charity, and the precious gift of final perseverance. Amen. | Ahabw'okuba ori Omukazi w'obutahikwaho kandi Nyina wa Ruhanga ow'amazima, tuhe omugisha gw'okurinda okwikiriza kwaitu, amatsiko g'omuhendo ahagati y'oburemeezi bw'amagara, okukunda kw'amaani, hamwe n'ekiconco ky'omuhendo eky'okugumisiriza. |
| Second Prayer | Okushaba kwa kabiri |
| O most gracious Virgin of Guadalupe, in your apparitions on Mount Tepeyac, you promised to show pity and compassion to all who, loving and trusting you, seek your help and protection. | Omweshongozi w'ekitiinisa omukazi wa Guadalupe, omu kureebeka kwawe aha rushozi Tepeyac, okaraganisa kugira embabazi n'okusaasira abo boona, abarikukunda kandi bakakwesiga, barikuronda obuhwezi bwawe n'oburinzi. |
| Listen to our supplications and grant us consolation and relief. We are full of hope that, relying on your help, nothing can trouble or affect us. As you have remained with us through your admirable image, so now obtain for us the graces we need. Amen. | Hurikiriza okushaba kwaitu kandi otuhe okuhuumurizibwa n'okuhuumura. Twine amatsiko ngu, turikwesiga obuhwezi bwawe, tihaine ekirikubaasa kututeganisa nainga kutukwataho. Nk'oku waagumire oine itwe kurabira omu kishushani kyawe ekirungi, hati otuhe embabazi ezi turikwetenga. Amen. |
| Our Lady of Fatima Novena Prayer | Okushaba kwa Our Lady of Fatima Novena |
| Our Lady of the Rosary of Fatima | Omukazi waitu w'ekikomo kya Fatima |
| Novena Prayer | Okushaba kwa Novena |
| Most Holy Virgin, who deigned to come to Fatima, to reveal the treasures of grace hidden in the recitation of the Rosary, inspire our hearts with a sincere love of this devotion, that meditating on the Mysteries of our Redemption recalled therein, we may obtain the conversion of sinners, the conversion of Russia, and (here name the other favors you are praying for), which we ask of you in this Novena, for the greater glory of God, for your own honor, and for the good of souls. Amen. | Omukazi Orikwera munonga, owaihireyo kwija Fatima, kushuuruura eby'obuguzi by'embabazi ebisherekirwe omu kushoma Rosari, atebekanise emitima yaitu n'okukunda okw'amazima okw'okwehayo oku, ngu okuteekateeka aha bintu ebirikwetengwa omu kuhindurwa kwaitu okurikugambwaho omuri kwo, nitubaasa kutunga okuhindurwa kw'abasiisi, okuhindurwa kwa Russia, hamwe (ahaheereze amaziina g'ebindi ebi orikushaba), ebi turikukushaba omu Novena egi, ahabw'ekitiinisa ky'amaani ekya Ruhanga, ahabw'ekitiinisa kyawe, hamwe n'oburungi bw'embeera z'abantu. Amen. |
| Our Lady of the Rosary of Fatima, Pray for us. | Omukama waitu w'ekishushani kya Rosary omuri Fatima, Tushabire. |
| The Canticle of Mary (Luke 1:46-55) | Ekishushani kya Mariamu (Luka 1:46-55) |
| The Canticle of Mary | Ekyeshongoro kya Mariamu |
| My soul proclaims the greatness of the Lord, my spirit rejoices in God, my savior, for he has looked with favor on his lowly servant. From this day all generations will call me blessed: the Almighty has done great things for me, and holy is his Name. | Omwoyo gwangye nigurangirira obukuru bwa Mukama, omutima gwangye nigushemererwa omuri Ruhanga, omujuni wangye, ahabw'okuba areebire omuheereza we owahansi n'embabazi. Okuruga aha eizooba eri emyaka yoona neija kumpeta nk'orikuhimbisibwa: Ruhanga ow'obushoboorozi bwona akozire ebintu bihango ahabwangye, kandi eiziina rye n'ery'ekitiinisa. |
| He has shown mercy on those who fear him in every generation. | Ayorekire embabazi aha barikumutiina omu buri murembe. |
| He has shown the strength of his arm, he has scattered the proud in their conceit. | Ayorekire amaani g'omukono gwe, ashansha abarikwetunguura omu kwetwara kubi. |
| He has cast down the mighty from their thrones, and has lifted up the lowly. | Akaiha ab'amaani aha ntebe zaabo, kandi yaheza yaayimusa abooro. |
| He has filled the hungry with good things, and the rich he has sent away empty. | Aijwiire abaine enjara ebirungi, kandi omugaiga amushendekyereize busha. |
| He has come to the help of his servant Israel for he has remembered his promise of mercy, the promise he made to our fathers, to Abraham and his children forever. | Yaizire kuhwera omuheereza we Israeli ahabw'okuba akaijuka okuraganisa kwe kw'embabazi, ekiraganiso eki yaakozire baishenkurwe, kuri Abrahamu n'abaana be ebiro byona. |
| Scripture Reference | Okuragiirwa kw'omu byahandiikirwe |
| Luke 1:46-55 | Luka 1:46-55 |
| "The Lord is my shepherd; I shall not want. He makes me lie down in green pastures." | "Mukama niwe mureeberezi wangye; tindikwija kwetenga. Naanyihaho omu byanya ebirikwiragura." |
| Psalm 23:1–2 | Zaaburi 23:1–2 |
| "Let all that you do be done in love." | Byona ebi murikukora, bikorwe omu kukunda |
| 1 Corinthians 16:14 | 1 Abakorinso 16:14 |
| Apostles' Creed | Okwikiriza kw'Abatume |
| I believe in God, the Father Almighty, Creator of Heaven and earth; and in Jesus Christ, His only Son, Our Lord, Who was conceived by the Holy Spirit, born of the Virgin Mary, suffered under Pontius Pilate, was crucified, died, and was buried. He descended into Hell; the third day He rose again from the dead; He ascended into Heaven, and sits at the right hand of God, the Father Almighty; from thence He shall come to judge the living and the dead. I believe in the Holy Spirit, the holy Catholic Church, the communion of saints, the forgiveness of sins, the resurrection of the body, and life everlasting. Amen. | Ninyikiriza omuri Ruhanga, Isheboona Omuhangi w'iguru n'ensi; kandi omuri Yesu Kristo, Omwana we wenka, Mukama waitu, Owaateirwe enda Omwoyo Orikwera, akazaarwa Mariamu Omwishiki Otaine Kibi, akabonabona ahansi ya Pontius Pilate, akakomererwa aha musharaba, yafa, kandi yaaziikwa. Akashuuma omu Heli; eizooba rya kashatu akagaruka yaazooka kuruga omu bafu; Akashuuma omu iguru, kandi ashutami aha mukono gwa buryo gwa Ruhanga, Isheboona Omuhangi w'iguru n'ensi; kuruga okwo naija kwija kucwa orubanja rw'abariho hamwe n'abafiire. Ninyikiriza omu Mwoyo Orikwera, Ekanisa y'Abakatuliki ey'oburungi, okukwatanisa kw'abarikwera, okusaasira ebibi, okuzooka kw'omubiri, hamwe n'amagara agatahwaho. Amen. |
| Our Father | Taata waitu |
| Our Father, Who art in heaven, hallowed be Thy name; Thy kingdom come; Thy will be done on earth as it is in heaven. Give us this day our daily bread; and forgive us our trespasses as we forgive those who trespass against us; and lead us not into temptation, but deliver us from evil. Amen. | Taata waitu, ori omu iguru, eiziina ryawe rihimbisibwe; obugabe bwawe buije; okukunda kwawe kukorwe ahansi nk'oku kuri omu iguru. Tuheereze eriizooba ebyokurya byaitu ebya buriizooba; kandi otusaasire ebibi byaitu nk'oku turikusaasira abo abarikutushobeza; kandi otatutwara omu kwohibwa, kureka otujune kuruga omu bibi. Amiina. |
| Hail Mary | Hail Mary |
| Hail Mary, full of grace, the Lord is with thee. Blessed art thou among women, and blessed is the fruit of thy womb, Jesus. Holy Mary, Mother of God, pray for us sinners, now and at the hour of our death. Amen. | Hail Mary, oijwire embabazi, Mukama ari naiwe. Oshemereirwe omu bakazi, kandi n'ekibari ky'enda yaawe nikishemererwa, Yesu. Mariya orikwera, Maama wa Ruhanga, shaba ahabwaitu abasiisi, hati hamwe n'omu shaaha y'okufa kwaitu. Amen. |
| Glory Be | Ekitiinisa kibe |
| Glory be to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit. As it was in the beginning, is now, and ever shall be, world without end. Amen. | Ekitiinisa kibe aha Taata, hamwe n'Omwana, hamwe n'Omwoyo Orikwera. Nk'oku kyabaire kiri omu kutandika, nikwo kiri hati, kandi nikiza kuguma kiri ebiro byona, ensi etaine muheru. Amen. |
| Fatima Prayer | Fatima Prayer |
| O my Jesus, forgive us our sins, save us from the fires of hell, lead all souls to Heaven, especially those in most need of Thy mercy. Amen. | O my Jesus, tusaasire ebibi byaitu, otujune omuriro gw'omuriro gw'omu kuzimu, oteere emyoyo yoona omu iguru, okukira munonga abo abarikwetenga embabazi zaawe. Amen. |
| Hail Holy Queen | Hail Holy Queen |
| Hail, Holy Queen, Mother of Mercy, our life, our sweetness and our hope! To thee do we cry, poor banished children of Eve; to thee do we send up our sighs, mourning and weeping in this valley of tears. Turn then, most gracious advocate, thine eyes of mercy toward us, and after this our exile, show unto us the blessed fruit of thy womb, Jesus. O clement, O loving, O sweet Virgin Mary! Pray for us, O Holy Mother of God, that we may be made worthy of the promises of Christ. Amen. | Hail, Holy Queen, Mother of Mercy, amagara gaitu, oburungi bwaitu hamwe n'amatsiko gaitu! Niwe turikweta, abaana ba Eve abooro ababingirwe; niwe turikwohereza okwetomboita kwaitu, okurira n'okurira omu kibungo eki eky'amarira. Hinduka, omurwanirizi w'embabazi, amaisho gaawe ag'embabazi gatuhwere, kandi bwanyima y'okubingwa kwaitu, tworekye ebijuma by'omukazi waawe ebirikugumizamu, Yesu. O clement, O loving, O sweet Virgin Mary! Tweshengyereze, O Holy Mother of God, ngu tukorwe abarikushemera ebiraganiso bya Kristo. Amen. |
| Daily Offering | Okuhayo buriizooba |
| O Jesus, through the Immaculate Heart of Mary, I offer you my prayers, works, joys and sufferings of this day, for all the intentions of your Sacred Heart, in union with the Holy Sacrifice of the Mass throughout the world, in reparation for sin, for the intentions of all my friends and associates, and in particular for the intentions of the Holy Father. | O Yesu, kurabira omu mutima gwa Mariya ogutarimu kibi, ninkuhayo okushaba kwangye, emirimo yangye, okushemererwa kwangye hamwe n'okubonabona kwangye okw'eizooba eri, ahabw'ebigyendererwa by'omutima gwawe ogurikwera, omu bumwe n'ekitambo ky'oburo bw'ekiro ky'okushaba omu nsi yoona, omu kugarukamu ekibi, ahabw'ebigyendererwa by'abanywani bangye boona hamwe n'abakwatanisa bangye, kandi okukira munonga ahabw'ebigyendererwa bya Paapa Orikwera. |
| My God, I believe in you and all that you have taught, because you have said it, and your word is true. | Ruhanga wangye, ninyikiriza omuri iwe hamwe n'ebi yashomesa byona, ahabw'okuba okabigamba kandi ekigambo kyawe kihikire. |
| I hope in your promise of Eternal life and I ask for your mercy and strength this day. | Ninteekateeka omu kiraganiso kyawe eky'amagara agatarikuhwaho kandi nimbashaba embabazi zaanyu hamwe n'amaani gaanyu eriizooba. |
| Act of Charity | Ekikorwa ky'embabazi |
| O My God, I love you with my whole heart and above all things, because you are infinitely good and perfect; and I love my neighbours as myself for love of you. Grant that I may love you more and more in this life, and in the next for all eternity. | O Ruhanga wangye, ninkukunda n'omutima gwangye gwona kandi okukira ebintu byona, ahabwokuba ori murungi munonga kandi ohikire; kandi ninkunda bataahi bangye nk'oku ndikwekunda ahabw'okukunda iwe. Nkweshengyereza ngu nkugume ninkukunda munonga omu magara aga, hamwe n'omu magara agandi kumara obwire bwona. |
| Morning Consecration Prayers | Okushaba kw'okuhayo omu kasheeshe |
| Merciful Jesus, I consecrate myself today and always to Your Most Sacred Heart. | Yesu ow'embabazi, eriizooba ninyikiriza kandi obutoosha ndikukuratira omutima gwawe ogurikwera. |
| Most Sacred Heart of Jesus I implore, that I may ever love you more and more. | Omutima gwa Yesu ogurikukirayo oburungi ninkushaba, ngu nyije nkakunda iwe okukira. |
| Most Sacred Heart of Jesus, I trust in you! | Omutima gwa Yesu ogurikukirayo oburungi, ninyesiga omuri iwe! |
| Most Sacred Heart of Jesus, have mercy on us! | Orikukirayo oburungi omu mutima gwa Yesu, tugirire embabazi! |
| Sacred Heart of Jesus, I believe in your love for me. | Omutima gurikwera ogwa Yesu, ninyikiriza omukukunda kwawe. |
| Jesus, meek and humble of heart, make my heart like your heart. | Yesu, omucureezi kandi ow'omutima gw'obucureezi, mpindura omutima gwangye nk'ogwe. |
| Prayer of Thanksgiving (Night) | Okushaba kw'okusiima (ekiro) |
| O My God, I thank you for all the benefits which I have received from you this day. Give me light to see what sins I have committed, and grant me grace to be truly sorry for them. | O Ruhanga wangye, ninkusiima ahabw'emigasho yoona ei ntungire kuruga ahariiwe eri izooba. Mpereza omushana gw'okureeba ebibi ebi ndikukora, kandi ompa embabazi z'okwetomboitwa ahabwabyo. |
| Act of Contrition (Night) | Ekikorwa ky'okweteisa (ekiro) |
| O my God, because you are so good, I am very sorry that I have sinned against you. With the help of your grace I will try not to sin again. | O Ruhanga wangye, ahabw'okuba ori murungi munonga, n'eky'obusaasi munonga ngu nkakora ekibi ahariiwe. Ahabw'obuhwezi bw'embabazi zaawe, ndaza kugyezaho kutagaruka kukora ekibi. |
| All Praise to You | Okuhimbisa kwona kuri ahariiwe |
| All praise to you, O God, this night for the blessings of the light. Keep us, we pray, O King of kings, Beneath your own almighty wings. | Okusiima kwona kuri ahariiwe, O Ruhanga, ekiro eki ahabw'emigisha y'omushana. Tugumize, nitushaba, O Mugabe w'abagabe, Ahansi y'amapapa gaawe ag'amaani. |
| Forgive us, Lord, through Christ Your Son, Whatever wrong this day we've done. Your peace give to the world, O Lord, That we might live in one accord. | Tusaasire, Mukama, kurabira omuri Kristo Omwana waawe, Ekibi kyona eki eizooba eri tukozire. Obusingye bwawe obuhe ensi yoona, Mukama, Kugira ngu tubaase kutuura hamwe omu busingye. |
| Enlighten us, O blessed light, And give us rest throughout this night. O strengthen us, that for your sake, We all may serve you when we wake. | Tuhwe omushana, owaitu ow'omugisha, Kandi otuhe obuhuumuro omu kiro eki. Otugumye, ngu ahabwawe, Twena tubaase kukukorera twaba tukeimuka. |
| The Angel of the Lord declared to Mary: And she conceived of the Holy Spirit. Hail Mary... | Maraika wa Mukama akagambira Mariamu ati: Kandi yaatwara enda ahabwa Omwoyo Orikwera. Hail Mary... |
| Behold the handmaid of the Lord: Be it done to me according to your word. Hail Mary... | Reeba omuzaana wa Mukama: Kikorebwe ahariinye nk'ekigambo kyawe. Hail Mary... |
| And the Word was made flesh: And dwelt among us. Hail Mary... | Kandi Ekigambo kikahindurwa omubiri: Kikatuura ahagati yaitu. Hail Mary... |
| Pray for us, O holy Mother of God. That we may be made worthy of the promise of Christ. | Tushabire, O maama orikwera wa Ruhanga. Kugira ngu tubaase kuhikiiriza ekiraganiso kya Kristo. |
| Let us pray: Pour forth, we beseech you, O Lord, your grace into our hearts that we, to whom the incarnation of Christ, your Son, was made known by the message of an angel, may by His passion and cross be brought to the glory of His resurrection, through the same Christ our Lord. Amen. | Reka tushabe: Twashaba, O Mukama, ota omugisha gwawe omu mitima yaitu ngu itwe, abu okuhinduka kwa Kristo, Omwana waawe, kwamanyisiibwe obutumwa bw'omumaraika, tubaase kurabira omu kubonabona kwe hamwe n'omusaraba kureetwa omu kitiinisa ky'okuzooka kwe, kurabira omuri Kristo ogwo Omukama waitu. Amiina. |
| O God, by the resurrection of Your Son, our Lord Jesus Christ, You have willed to make glad the whole world. Grant, we beseech You, that through His Mother, the Virgin Mary, we may lay hold of the joys of eternal life. We ask this through the same Christ our Lord. Amen. | Ai Ruhanga, ahabw'okuzooka kw'Omwana waawe, Mukama waitu Yesu Kristo, oshaziremu kushemeza ensi yoona. Twakushaba ngu, kurabira omuri nyina we, Omukazi Mariya, tubone kukwata okushemererwa kw'amagara agatarikuhwaho. Eki tukashaba kurabira omuri Kristo Mukama waitu. Amiina. |
| Divine Mercy Chaplet (3 O'Clock Prayer) | Ekirunga ky'embabazi za Ruhanga (Okushaba kwa shaaha ishatu) |
| You expired Jesus, but the source of life gushed forth for souls, and the ocean of mercy opened up for the whole world. O Fount of Life, unfathomable Divine Mercy, envelop the whole world and empty Yourself out upon us. | Okahwaho Yesu, kwonka oburugo bw'amagara bukarugamu amagara g'emyoyo, kandi enyanja y'embabazi ekiguruka ahabw'ensi yoona. O Fount of Life, embabazi za Ruhanga ezitarikwetegyerezibwa, shwekyera ensi yoona kandi oyeyongyere ahariitwe. |
| O Blood and Water which gushed forth from the Heart of Jesus as a fount of mercy for us, I trust in you! | Oshagama n'amaizi ebyarugire omu mutima gwa Yesu nk'ensibuko y'embabazi ahariitwe, ninyesiga omuriimwe! |
| Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have Mercy on us and on the whole world. (Say 3 times) Amen. | Ruhanga orikwera, Omunyamaani orikwera, Orikutafa orikwera, Twagirire embabazi hamwe n'ensi yoona. (Gamba emirundi eshatu) Amen. |
| Leader: Our Father... All together: Give us this day... Leader: Hail Mary... All together: Holy Mary... | Omwebembezi: Tataitwe... Twena hamwe: Otuhe ebiro ebi... Omwebembezi: Hail Mary... Twena hamwe: Mariya orikwera... |
| All together pray the Creed: I believe in God, the Father almighty, the creator of heaven and earth and in Jesus Christ, His only Son, Our Lord, who was conceived by the Holy Spirit, born of the Virgin Mary, suffered under Pontius Pilate, was crucified, died and was buried. He descended into hell. On the third day He rose again from the dead. He ascended into heaven and sits at the right hand of God, the Father almighty. From thence He shall come to judge the living and the dead. I believe in the Holy Spirit, the holy Catholic Church, the Communion of Saints, the forgiveness of sins, the resurrection of the body and life everlasting. Amen. | Buri omwe ashabire ekiragiro ky'okwikiriza: Ninyikiriza omuri Ruhanga, Isheboona ow'obushoboorozi bwona, owaahangire eiguru n'ensi hamwe na Yesu Kristo, Omwana we wenka, Mukama waitu, owaateekateekirweho Omwoyo Orikwera, akazaarwa omukazi w'omwishiki Mariamu, akabonabona ahansi ya Pontius Pilate, akakomererwa aha musaraba, yaafa kandi yaaziikwa. Akarugayo yaaza omu hell. Aha eizooba rya kashatu akagaruka yaazooka kuruga omu bafu. Akatemba omu iguru kandi ashutama aha mukono gwa buryo gwa Ruhanga, Isheboona ow'obushoboorozi bwona. Kuruga okwo naija kwija kucwera abahuriire hamwe n'abafu orubanja. Ninyikiriza omu Mwoyo Orikwera, Ekanisa y'Abakatuliki ey'oburungi, Okuhikaana kw'Abatukuvu, okusaasirwa ebibi, okuzooka kw'omubiri hamwe n'amagara agatarikuhwaho. Amen. |
| (On the large bead before each decade) All together: Eternal Father, I offer you the Body and Blood, Soul and Divinity of Your dearly beloved Son, Our Lord Jesus Christ in atonement for our sins and those of the whole world. | (Oku omukondo muhango oguri omu maisho ga buri mwaka) Twena hamwe: Taata ow'obutwire, ninkuhayo omubiri gwawe hamwe n'esagama, omutima hamwe n'oburungi bw'omwana waawe ou orikukunda munonga, Mukama waitu Yesu Kristo omu kushashura ebibi byaitu hamwe n'eby'ensi yoona. |
| (On the ten small beads of each decade) Leader: For the sake of His sorrowful passion All together: Have mercy on us and on the whole world. | (Oku ebikomo ikumi bikye bya buri kumi) Omwebembezi: Ahabw'okusaasira kw'okushaba kwe Twena hamwe: Tugirire embabazi n'ensi yoona. |
| (Conclude with): Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have mercy on us and on the whole world. (say three times) Amen. Jesus, King of Mercy, I trust in you! (say 3 times) | (Okuhendera n'okugira): Ruhanga orikwera, Omunyamaani Orikwera, Orikutahwaho, Twagirire embabazi hamwe n'ensi yoona. (gamba emirundi eshatu) Amen. Yesu, Omugabe w'embabazi, Ninkwesiga! (gamba emirundi eshatu) |
| Note: The first line appears twice as it is to be read twice. | Eky'okureeberaho: Orunyiriri rw'okubanza nirworeka emirundi ebiri ahabwokuba nirushemereire kushomwa emirundi ebiri. |
| Say 13 Our Father's and 13 Hail Mary's for 13 consecutive days. Publish this prayer, share it with a friend and give a Mass of Thanksgiving. | Gamba 13 Our Father's na 13 Hail Mary's ahabw'ebiro 13 ebirikukurataho. Shohoza okushaba oku, ogabane n'omunywani kandi oheereze Misa y'okusiima. |
| Our Lady of Guadalupe, mystical rose, make intercession for the Holy Church; protect the Holy Father; help all those who invoke you in their needs. | Mukama waitu wa Guadalupe, orushozi rw'omumisho, okuhurikiza omushango gw'ekanisa erikwera; okurinda isheboona ow'erikwera; okuyamba boona abarikukushaba omubyetengo byabo. |
| O most gracious Virgin of Guadalupe, in your apparitions on Mount Tepeyac, you promised to show pity and compassion to all who, loving and trusting you, seek your help and protection. Listen to our supplications and grant us consolation and relief. We are full of hope that, relying on your help, nothing can trouble or affect us. As you have remained with us through your admirable image, so now obtain for us the graces we need. Amen. | Omwishiki w'omugisha gw'amaani owa Guadalupe, omu kureebeka kwawe aha rushozi Tepeyac, okaraganisa kworeka embabazi n'okusaasira abo boona, barikukunda kandi barikukwesiga, abarikuronda obuhwezi bwawe n'oburinzi. Hurikiriza okushaba kwaitu kandi otuhe okuhuumurizibwa n'okuhuumura. Twine amatsiko ngu, turikwesiga obuhwezi bwawe, tihaine ekirikubaasa kutubonabonesa nainga kututeganisa. Nk'oku waagumire naitwe kurabira omu kishushani kyawe ekirikutangaaza, hati otuhe emigisha ei turikwetenga. Amen. |
| Most Holy Virgin, who deigned to come to Fatima, to reveal the treasures of grace hidden in the recitation of the Rosary, inspire our hearts with a sincere love of this devotion, that meditating on the Mysteries of our Redemption recalled therein, we may obtain the conversion of sinners, the conversion of Russia, and (here name the other favors you are praying for) which we ask of you in this Novena, for the greater glory of God, for your own honor, and for the good of souls. Amen. | Omweshongozi w'ekitiinisa ky'amaani, owaikiriza kwija Fatima, kushoboorora eby'obugaiga bw'embabazi ebisherekirwe omu kushoma Rosary, akahwera emitima yaitu n'okukunda okw'amazima okw'okuramya oku, okurikuteekateeka aha bintu by'omutaano eby'okucungurwa kwaitu ebirikugambwaho omuriyo, nitubaasa kutunga okuhinduka kw'abasiisi, okuhinduka kwa Russia, hamwe (ahaheeru y'aha, gamba aha migisha endiijo ei orikushaba) ei turikukushaba omu Novena egi, ahabw'ekitiinisa kya Ruhanga ekirikukirayo, ahabw'ekitiinisa kyawe, hamwe n'oburungi bw'emwoyo. Amen. |
| Our Lady of the Rosary of Fatima, pray for us. | Omukama waitu w'ekishushani kya rosari omuri Fatima, tushabire. |
| My soul proclaims the greatness of the Lord, my spirit rejoices in God, my savior, for he has looked with favor on his lowly servant. From this day all generations will call me blessed: the Almighty has done great things for me, and holy is his Name. | Omwoyo gwangye nigurangirira obukuru bwa Mukama, omutima gwangye nigushemererwa omuri Ruhanga, omujuni wangye, ahabw'okuba areebire omuheereza we owahansi n'embabazi. Okuruga aha eizooba eri emigigi yoona neija kumpeta nk'orikweziibwa: Ruhanga ow'obushoboorozi bwona akozire ebintu bihango ahariinye, kandi eiziina rye niritukura. |
| He has shown mercy on those who fear him in every generation. | Ayorekire embabazi aha barikumutiina omu buri murembe. |
| He has shown the strength of his arm, he has scattered the proud in their conceit. | Ayorekire amaani g'omukono gwe, ashanswire abarikwetunguura omu kwetwara nk'ab'amaani. |
| He has cast down the mighty from their thrones, and has lifted up the lowly. | Akaiha ab'amaani aha ntebe zaabo, kandi akatwara abooro omu maisho. |
| He has filled the hungry with good things, and the rich he has sent away empty. | Aijwiire abaine enjara n'ebintu birungi, kandi omugaiga amushendekyereize busha. |
| He has come to the help of his servant Israel for he has remembered his promise of mercy, the promise he made to our fathers, to Abraham and his children forever. | Yaizire kuhwera omuheereza we Israeli ahabw'okuba akaijuka ekiraganiso kye ky'embabazi, ekiraganiso eki yaakozire aha baisheboitwe, aha Abrahamu hamwe n'abaana be ebiro byona. |
| (Luke 1:46-55) | (Luka 1:46-55) |
| Litany of the Blessed Virgin Mary | Ekitabo ky'omukazi w'ekitiinisa Mariya |
| Lord, have mercy. Lord, have mercy. Christ, have mercy. Christ, have mercy. Lord, have mercy. Lord, have mercy. Christ, hear us. Christ, graciously hear us. | Mukama, gira embabazi. Mukama, gira embabazi. Kristo, gira embabazi. Kristo, gira embabazi. Mukama, gira embabazi. Mukama, gira embabazi. Kristo, tuhurire. Kristo, tuhurire omu muringo gw'embabazi. |
| God the Father of Heaven, have mercy on us. God the Son, Redeemer of the world, have mercy on us. God the Holy Spirit, have mercy on us. Holy Trinity, one God, have mercy on us. | Ruhanga Isheboona, omutegyeki w'iguru, twagirire embabazi. Ruhanga Omwana, omucunguzi w'ensi, twagirire embabazi. Ruhanga Omwoyo Orikwera, twagirire embabazi. Obutatu Oburikwera, Ruhanga omwe, twagirire embabazi. |
| Holy Mary, pray for us. Holy Mother of God, pray for us. Holy Virgin of virgins, pray for us. Mother of Christ, pray for us. Mother of the Church, pray for us. Mother of Divine Grace, pray for us. Mother most pure, pray for us. Mother most chaste, pray for us. Mother inviolate, pray for us. Mother undefiled, pray for us. Mother most amiable, pray for us. Mother most admirable, pray for us. Mother of good counsel, pray for us. Mother of our Creator, pray for us. Mother of our Savior, pray for us. | Mariya orikwera, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama w'omwana wa Ruhanga, shaba ahabwaitu. Omukazi w'ekitiinisa, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama wa Kristo, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama w'ekanisa, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama w'embabazi z'obuhangwa, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama owurikwera munonga, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama owurikwetegyerezibwa munonga, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama otarikubaasa kuhenda, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama otarikushishwa, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama orikukirayo kushemeza, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama orikukirayo kusiimwa, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama w'okuteekateeka okurungi, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama w'omuhandiiki waitu, shaba ahabwaitu. Maama w'omujuni waitu, shaba ahabwaitu. |
| Virgin most prudent, pray for us. Virgin most venerable, pray for us. Virgin most renowned, pray for us. Virgin most powerful, pray for us. Virgin most merciful, pray for us. Virgin most faithful, pray for us. | Omukazi w'obusugi ow'obwengye, mushabe ahabwaitu. Omukazi w'obusugi ow'ekitiinisa, mushabe ahabwaitu. Omukazi w'obusugi ow'amaani, mushabe ahabwaitu. Omukazi w'obusugi ow'embabazi, mushabe ahabwaitu. Omukazi w'obusugi ow'amazima, mushabe ahabwaitu. |
| Mirror of justice, pray for us. Seat of wisdom, pray for us. Cause of our joy, pray for us. Spiritual vessel, pray for us. Vessel of honor, pray for us. Singular vessel of devotion, pray for us. Mystical rose, pray for us. Tower of David, pray for us. Tower of ivory, pray for us. House of gold, pray for us. Ark of the covenant, pray for us. Gate of heaven, pray for us. Morning star, pray for us. Health of the sick, pray for us. Refuge of sinners, pray for us. Comfort of the afflicted, pray for us. Help of Christians, pray for us. | Ekirahuri ky'oburingaaniza, shaba ahabwaitu. Entebe y'obwengye, shaba ahabwaitu. Enshonga y'okushemererwa kwaitu, shaba ahabwaitu. Ekintu ky'omwoyo, shaba ahabwaitu. Ekintu ky'ekitiinisa, shaba ahabwaitu. Ekintu kimwe ky'okwehayo, shaba ahabwaitu. Roses ey'ekihama, shaba ahabwaitu. Omunaara gwa Davidi, shaba ahabwaitu. Omunaara gw'amahembe, shaba ahabwaitu. Enju ya zaabu, shaba ahabwaitu. Ekyombeko ky'endagaano, shaba ahabwaitu. Omuryango gw'omu iguru, shaba ahabwaitu. Enyonyoozi y'akasheeshe, shaba ahabwaitu. Amagara g'abarwaire, shaba ahabwaitu. Obuhungiro bw'abasiisi, shaba ahabwaitu. Okuhuumurizibwa kw'ababonabona, shaba ahabwaitu. Obuhwezi bw'Abakurisitaayo, shaba ahabwaitu. |
| Queen of Angels, pray for us. Queen of Patriarchs, pray for us. Queen of Prophets, pray for us. Queen of Apostles, pray for us. Queen of Martyrs, pray for us. Queen of Confessors, pray for us. Queen of Virgins, pray for us. Queen of all Saints, pray for us. Queen conceived without original sin, pray for us. Queen assumed into heaven, pray for us. Queen of the most holy Rosary, pray for us. Queen of families, pray for us. Queen of peace, pray for us. | Omugabekazi w'Abamaraika, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'Abakuru b'ediini, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'Abaporofita, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'Abatume, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'Abajurizi, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'Abareeberezi, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'Abashugaine, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'Abashugaine boona, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi owazairwe ataine kibi ky'okubanza, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi owatwaire omu iguru, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'omusaraba ogurikwera munonga, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'amaka, shaba ahabwaitu. Omugabekazi w'obusingye, shaba ahabwaitu. |
| Lamb of God, who takes away the sins of the world, spare us, O Lord. Lamb of God, who takes away the sins of the world, graciously hear us, O Lord. Lamb of God, who takes away the sins of the world, have mercy on us. | Omwana gw'entaama ogwa Ruhanga, orikwihaho ebibi by'ensi, otuhumure, Mukama. Omwana gw'entaama ogwa Ruhanga, orikwihaho ebibi by'ensi, otuhurire n'embabazi, Mukama. Omwana gw'entaama ogwa Ruhanga, orikwihaho ebibi by'ensi, ogyende omu maisho kutugirira embabazi. |
| Pray for us, O Holy Mother of God. That we may be made worthy of the promises of Christ. | Tushabire, Nyina Ruhanga Orikwera. Kugira ngu tubaase kuhikiiriza ebiraganiso bya Kristo. |
| Let us pray: Grant, we beseech you, O Lord God, that we your servants may enjoy perpetual health of mind and body; and by the intercession of the Blessed Mary, ever Virgin, may be delivered from present sorrow, and obtain eternal joy. Through Christ our Lord. Amen. | Ka tushabe: Twakushaba, Mukama Ruhanga, ngu abahuuku baawe tubaase kugira amagara g'omutima n'omubiri agatarikuhwaho; kandi kurabira omu kuhurikiriza kwa Mariya ow'ekitiinisa, Omukazi w'obusugi obutarikuhwaho, tubaase kujunwa okusaasibwa kw'obwahati, kandi tutungye okushemererwa okutahwaho. Kurabira omuri Kristo Mukama waitu. Amen. |

| Focus on Jesus proclaiming the Kingdom of God. | Céntrate en Jesús proclamando el Reino de Dios. |
| Focus on Jesus being transfigured on Mount Tabor. | Céntrate en la transfiguración de Jesús en el Monte Tabor. |
| Focus on Jesus instituting the Holy Eucharist. | Céntrate en cómo Jesús instituyó la Sagrada Eucaristía. |
| Jesus is condemned to death | Condenan a muerte a Jesús |
| Pilate hands Jesus over to be crucified despite knowing He is innocent. | Pilato entrega a Jesús para que lo crucifiquen, a pesar de saber que es inocente. |
| Jesus takes up His Cross | Jesús carga con su cruz |
| Jesus accepts the heavy cross, bearing the weight of our sins with love. | Jesús acepta la pesada cruz y carga con el peso de nuestros pecados con amor. |
| Jesus falls the first time | Jesús se cae por primera vez |
| Weakened by suffering, Jesus falls, yet rises to continue His journey for us. | Debilitado por el sufrimiento, Jesús se cae, pero se levanta para seguir su camino por nosotros. |
| Jesus meets His Mother | Jesús se encuentra con su madre |
| A moment of profound sorrow as Mary sees her Son carrying the cross. | Un momento de profunda tristeza cuando María ve a su Hijo cargando con la cruz. |
| Simon of Cyrene helps Jesus | Simón de Cirene ayuda a Jesús |
| Simon is compelled to help, reminding us to carry each other's burdens. | Simón no puede evitar echar una mano, lo que nos recuerda que debemos llevar las cargas los unos de los otros. |
| Veronica wipes the face of Jesus | Verónica le seca la cara a Jesús |
| An act of compassion leaves the image of Christ's face on her veil. | Un gesto de compasión deja la imagen del rostro de Cristo en su velo. |
| Jesus falls the second time | Jesús se cae por segunda vez |
| Jesus falls again, showing the depth of His suffering and love for us. | Jesús vuelve a caer, lo que muestra lo profundo que es su sufrimiento y su amor por nosotros. |
| Jesus meets the women of Jerusalem | Jesús se encuentra con las mujeres de Jerusalén |
| Jesus consoles the weeping women, calling us to weep for our sins. | Jesús consuela a las mujeres que lloran y nos invita a llorar por nuestros pecados. |
| Jesus falls the third time | Jesús se cae por tercera vez |
| Near Calvary, Jesus falls once more, yet His love does not waver. | Cerca del Calvario, Jesús se cae una vez más, pero su amor no flaquea. |
| Jesus is stripped of His garments | A Jesús le quitan la ropa |
| Jesus is humiliated, stripped of all dignity, for our redemption. | Jesús es humillado, despojado de toda dignidad, por nuestra redención. |
| Jesus is nailed to the Cross | Jesús está clavado en la cruz |
| The nails pierce His hands and feet, driven by our sins and held by His love. | Los clavos le atraviesan las manos y los pies, clavados por nuestros pecados y sujetos por Su amor. |
| Jesus dies on the Cross | Jesús muere en la cruz |
| "It is finished." The ultimate sacrifice is made for all of humanity. | «Ya está hecho». Se ha realizado el sacrificio definitivo por toda la humanidad. |
| Jesus is taken down from the Cross | Descienden a Jesús de la cruz |
| Mary receives the body of her Son: a mother's grief beyond words. | María recibe el cuerpo de su Hijo: el dolor de una madre que no se puede expresar con palabras. |
| Jesus is laid in the tomb | Ponen a Jesús en el sepulcro |
| The tomb is sealed, but death does not have the final word. | La tumba está sellada, pero la muerte no tiene la última palabra. |

| Trust in the ocean of Divine Mercy | Confía en el océano de la Misericordia Divina |

| The Three O'clock Prayer | La oración de las tres en punto |
| Opening Prayer | Oración inicial |
| All together pray: | Recemos todos juntos: |
| You expired Jesus, But the source of life gushed forth for souls, and the ocean of mercy opened up for the whole world O Fount of Life, unfathomable Divine Mercy, envelop the whole world and empty Yourself out upon us. | Tú falleciste, Jesús, pero la fuente de la vida brotó para las almas, y el océano de la misericordia se abrió para todo el mundo. Oh, Fuente de Vida, insondable Misericordia Divina, envuelve al mundo entero y derrámate sobre nosotros. |
| O Blood and Water | Oh, sangre y agua |
| O Blood and Water which gushed forth from the Heart of Jesus as a fount of mercy for us, I trust in you! | ¡Oh, Sangre y Agua, que brotasteis del Corazón de Jesús como una fuente de misericordia para nosotros, confío en vosotros! |
| Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have Mercy on us and on the whole world. (Say 3 times) Amen. | Dios Santo, Poderoso y Inmortal, ten piedad de nosotros y de todo el mundo. (Repítelo 3 veces) Amén. |
| Jesus, King of Mercy, I trust in you! | ¡Jesús, Rey de la Misericordia, confío en ti! |
| Our Father & Hail Mary | El «Padre Nuestro» y el «Ave María» |
| Leader: Our Father… All together: Give us this day… | Guía: Padre nuestro… Todos juntos: Danos hoy… |
| Leader: Hail Mary… All together: Holy Mary… | Guía: Ave María… Todos juntos: Santa María… |
| The Apostles' Creed | El Credo de los Apóstoles |
| All together pray the Creed: | Recemos todos juntos el Credo: |
| I believe in God, the Father almighty, the creator of heaven and earth and in Jesus Christ, His only Son, Our Lord, who was conceived by the Holy Spirit, born of the Virgin Mary, suffered under Pontius Pilate, was crucified, died and was buried. He descended into hell. On the third day He rose again from the dead. He ascended into heaven and sits at the right hand of God, the Father almighty. From thence He shall come to judge the living and the dead. I believe in the Holy Spirit, the holy Catholic Church, the Communion of Saints, the forgiveness of sins, the resurrection of the body and life everlasting. Amen. | Creo en Dios, el Padre todopoderoso, creador del cielo y de la tierra, y en Jesucristo, su único Hijo, nuestro Señor, que fue concebido por el Espíritu Santo, nació de la Virgen María, padeció bajo el mandato de Poncio Pilato, fue crucificado, murió y fue sepultado. Descendió a los infiernos. Al tercer día resucitó de entre los muertos. Subió al cielo y está sentado a la derecha de Dios, el Padre todopoderoso. De allí vendrá a juzgar a los vivos y a los muertos. Creo en el Espíritu Santo, en la santa Iglesia católica, en la comunión de los santos, en el perdón de los pecados, en la resurrección de la carne y en la vida eterna. Amén. |
| On the Large Bead Before Each Decade | En la cuenta grande que hay antes de cada década |
| All together: Eternal Father, I offer you the Body and Blood, Soul and Divinity of Your dearly beloved Son, Our Lord Jesus Christ in atonement for our sins and those of the whole world. | Todos juntos: Padre Eterno, te ofrezco el Cuerpo y la Sangre, el Alma y la Divinidad de tu amadísimo Hijo, nuestro Señor Jesucristo, en expiación por nuestros pecados y los del mundo entero. |
| On the Ten Small Beads of Each Decade | Sobre las diez cuentas pequeñas de cada década |
| Leader: For the sake of His sorrowful passion All together: Have mercy on us and on the whole world. | Líder: Por su dolorosa pasión Todos juntos: Ten piedad de nosotros y del mundo entero. |
| Concluding Prayer | Oración final |
| Holy God, Holy Mighty One, Holy Immortal One, Have mercy on us and on the whole world. (Say three times) Amen. | Dios Santo, Poderoso y Inmortal, ten piedad de nosotros y de todo el mundo. (Repítelo tres veces) Amén. |
| Jesus, King of Mercy, I trust in you! (say 3 times) | ¡Jesús, Rey de la Misericordia, confío en ti! (dilo 3 veces) |
| Pray these prayers once a day for 9 days | Reza estas oraciones una vez al día durante 9 días |
| How to Pray This Novena | Cómo rezar esta novena |
| Pray the same prayers each day, for 9 days in a row. | Reza las mismas oraciones cada día, durante 9 días seguidos. |
| Opening | Apertura |
| In the Name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit. Amen. | En el nombre del Padre, del Hijo y del Espíritu Santo. Amén. |
| Litany of Our Lady of Perpetual Help | Letanía de Nuestra Señora del Perpetuo Socorro |
| Lord, have mercy on us. Christ, have mercy on us. | Señor, ten piedad de nosotros. Cristo, ten piedad de nosotros. |
| Lord, have mercy on us. Christ, hear us. Christ, graciously hear us. | Señor, ten piedad de nosotros. Cristo, escúchanos. Cristo, escúchanos con misericordia. |
| God the Father of Heaven, Have mercy on us. | Dios, Padre del Cielo, ten piedad de nosotros. |
| God the Son, Redeemer of the world, Have mercy on us. | Dios Hijo, Redentor del mundo, ten piedad de nosotros. |
| God the Holy Ghost, Have mercy on us. | Dios, el Espíritu Santo, ten piedad de nosotros. |
| Holy Trinity, One God, Have mercy on us. | Santísima Trinidad, único Dios, ten piedad de nosotros. |
| Holy Mary, pray for us. | Santa María, ruega por nosotros. |
| Holy Mother of God, pray for us. | Santa Madre de Dios, ruega por nosotros. |
